	// NEW: разделение по направлению

	OutgoingConns int
	SynRecv       int
	SynSent       int
	LocalPorts    map[int]struct{} // наши порты, на которые стучатся
}

}

}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/filter.go
================================================================================
package parser

import (
	"net"
	"strings"
)

type FilterConfig struct {
	// Whitelist — адреса и подсети, которые ИСКЛЮЧАЮТСЯ из мониторинга.
	// Это "known-safe" источники, а не "всегда мониторить".
	// Поддерживаются одиночные IP и CIDR.
	Whitelist []string

	IgnorePrivate     bool
	IgnoreLoopback    bool
	IgnoreLinkLocal   bool
	IgnoreUnspecified bool
}

type Filter struct {
	whitelistIPs  map[string]struct{}
	whitelistNets []*net.IPNet

	ignorePrivate     bool
	ignoreLoopback    bool
	ignoreLinkLocal   bool
	ignoreUnspecified bool
}

func NewFilter(cfg FilterConfig) Filter {
	ips := make(map[string]struct{}, len(cfg.Whitelist))
	var nets []*net.IPNet

	for _, entry := range cfg.Whitelist {
		entry = strings.TrimSpace(entry)
		if entry == "" {
			continue
		}

		if _, ipnet, err := net.ParseCIDR(entry); err == nil {
			nets = append(nets, ipnet)
			continue
		}

		if ip := net.ParseIP(entry); ip != nil {
			ips[ip.String()] = struct{}{}
			continue
		}

		// Невалидные записи молча отбрасываем — они всё равно не сработают.
		// Логирование здесь невозможно без логгера; если нужен warning —
		// верните error из NewFilter и обработайте в app.go.
	}

	return Filter{
		whitelistIPs:      ips,
		whitelistNets:     nets,
		ignorePrivate:     cfg.IgnorePrivate,
		ignoreLoopback:    cfg.IgnoreLoopback,
		ignoreLinkLocal:   cfg.IgnoreLinkLocal,
		ignoreUnspecified: cfg.IgnoreUnspecified,
	}
}

// ShouldInclude возвращает true, если соединение нужно учитывать.
//
// Порядок проверок:
//  1. Пустой IP или порт <= 0 → false.
//  2. Невалидный IP → false.
//  3. Whitelist (IP или CIDR) → false (исключаем из мониторинга).
//  4. Multicast → false (всегда).
//  5. ignoreUnspecified / ignoreLoopback / ignoreLinkLocal / ignorePrivate → false.
//
// Локальный IP в фильтрации не участвует (оставлен для совместимости сигнатуры).
func (f Filter) ShouldInclude(_, remoteIP string, remotePort int) bool {
	if remoteIP == "" || remotePort <= 0 {
		return false
	}

	ip := net.ParseIP(remoteIP)
	if ip == nil {
		return false
	}

	if f.isWhitelisted(ip) {
		return false
	}

	if ip.IsMulticast() {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignoreUnspecified && ip.IsUnspecified() {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignoreLoopback && ip.IsLoopback() {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignoreLinkLocal && (ip.IsLinkLocalUnicast() || ip.IsLinkLocalMulticast()) {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignorePrivate && ip.IsPrivate() {
		return false
	}

	return true
}

func (f Filter) isWhitelisted(ip net.IP) bool {
	if len(f.whitelistIPs) > 0 {
		if _, ok := f.whitelistIPs[ip.String()]; ok {
			return true
		}
	}

	for _, n := range f.whitelistNets {
		if n.Contains(ip) {
			return true
		}
	}

	return false
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/hex.go
================================================================================
package parser

import "strconv"

// hexByte парсит один байт в hex-виде (2 символа).
// Возвращает (значение, true) при успехе.
func hexByte(s string) (byte, bool) {
	if len(s) != 2 {
		return 0, false
	}

	v, err := strconv.ParseUint(s, 16, 8)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, false
	}

	return byte(v), true
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/ipv4.go
================================================================================
package parser

import "fmt"

func ParseIPv4Hex(s string) (string, bool) {
	if len(s) != 8 {
		return "", false
	}

	b0, ok0 := hexByte(s[6:8])
	b1, ok1 := hexByte(s[4:6])
	b2, ok2 := hexByte(s[2:4])
	b3, ok3 := hexByte(s[0:2])

	if !ok0 || !ok1 || !ok2 || !ok3 {
		return "", false
	}

	return fmt.Sprintf("%d.%d.%d.%d", b0, b1, b2, b3), true
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/ipv6.go
================================================================================
package parser

import "net"

func ParseIPv6Hex(s string) (string, bool) {
	if len(s) != 32 {
		return "", false
	}

	raw := make(net.IP, 16)

	for group := 0; group < 4; group++ {
		word := s[group*8 : (group+1)*8]

		for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
			start := (3 - i) * 2

			b, ok := hexByte(word[start : start+2])
			if !ok {
				return "", false
			}

			raw[group*4+i] = b
		}
	}

	if v4 := raw.To4(); v4 != nil {
		return v4.String(), true
	}

	return raw.String(), true
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/proc.go
================================================================================
package parser

import (
	"bufio"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

const (
	ProcTCP  = "/proc/net/tcp"
	ProcTCP6 = "/proc/net/tcp6"

	// EphemeralPortMin — нижняя граница динамических портов Linux.
	// cat /proc/sys/net/ipv4/ip_local_port_range → обычно 32768.
	EphemeralPortMin = 32768
)

// Config управления разбором /proc/net/tcp{,6}.
type ProcConfig struct {
	KeepIPv6 bool
}

func DefaultProcConfig() ProcConfig {
	return ProcConfig{KeepIPv6: true}
}

func DefaultProcPaths() []string {
	return []string{ProcTCP, ProcTCP6}
}

func ParseProc(paths []string, filter Filter) ([]*models.Connection, error) {
	return ParseProcWithConfig(paths, filter, DefaultProcConfig())
}

func ParseProcWithConfig(
	paths []string,
	filter Filter,
	cfg ProcConfig,
) ([]*models.Connection, error) {
	var conns []*models.Connection
	parsed := false
	for _, path := range paths {
		found, err := parseOneProcFile(path, filter, cfg, &conns)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if found {
			parsed = true
		}
	}
	if !parsed {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no /proc/net/tcp files were parsed")
	}
	return conns, nil
}

func parseOneProcFile(
	path string,
	filter Filter,
	cfg ProcConfig,
	out *[]*models.Connection,
) (found bool, err error) {
	// #nosec G304 -- paths are hardcoded /proc/net/tcp{,6}
	file, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return false, nil
		}
		return false, fmt.Errorf("open %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()

	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 1024*1024), 1024*1024)

	for scanner.Scan() {
		fields := strings.Fields(scanner.Text())
		if len(fields) < 4 {
			continue
		}
		if fields[0] == "sl" {
			continue
		}

		localIP, localPort, _, ok := parseAddress(fields[1])
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		remoteIP, remotePort, remoteIsV4, ok := parseAddress(fields[2])
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if remotePort <= 0 {
			continue
		}
		if !cfg.KeepIPv6 && !remoteIsV4 {
			continue
		}

		conn := &models.Connection{
			LocalIP:    localIP,
			LocalPort:  localPort,
			RemoteIP:   remoteIP,
			RemotePort: remotePort,
			State:      tcpState(fields[3]),
		}
		classifyDirection(conn)

		if filter.ShouldInclude(localIP, remoteIP, remotePort) {
			*out = append(*out, conn)
		}
	}
	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("scan %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	return true, nil
}

// classifyDirection определяет направление соединения.
//
// Правила:
//   - SYN_RECV  → всегда входящее (кто-то стучится к нам).
//   - SYN_SENT  → всегда исходящее (мы стучимся наружу).
//   - Остальные → по эпемерным портам:
//     LocalPort ephemeral + RemotePort сервисный  → мы клиент (исходящее).
//     RemotePort ephemeral + LocalPort сервисный  → мы сервер (входящее).
//     Оба высокие или оба низкие → не помечаем (P2P/unknown).
func classifyDirection(c *models.Connection) {
	switch c.State {
	case "SYN_RECV":
		c.IsIncoming = true
	case "SYN_SENT":
		c.IsOutgoing = true
	case "ESTABLISHED", "FIN_WAIT1", "FIN_WAIT2", "TIME_WAIT",
		"CLOSE_WAIT", "LAST_ACK", "CLOSING":
		localEph := c.LocalPort >= EphemeralPortMin
		remoteEph := c.RemotePort >= EphemeralPortMin
		if localEph && !remoteEph {
			c.IsOutgoing = true
		} else if remoteEph && !localEph {
			c.IsIncoming = true
		}
		// Оба высокие или оба низкие → не помечаем.
	}
}

func parseAddress(s string) (ip string, port int, ipv4 bool, ok bool) {
	parts := strings.SplitN(s, ":", 2)
	if len(parts) != 2 {
		return "", 0, false, false
	}
	port64, err := strconv.ParseUint(parts[1], 16, 16)
	if err != nil {
		return "", 0, false, false
	}
	hexIP := parts[0]
	switch len(hexIP) {
	case 8:
		ipv4Str, okv4 := ParseIPv4Hex(hexIP)
		if !okv4 {
			return "", 0, false, false
		}
		return ipv4Str, int(port64), true, true
	case 32:
		ipStr, okv6 := ParseIPv6Hex(hexIP)
		if !okv6 {
			return "", 0, false, false
		}
		parsed := net.ParseIP(ipStr)
		if parsed == nil {
			return "", 0, false, false
		}
		return ipStr, int(port64), parsed.To4() != nil, true
	default:
		return "", 0, false, false
	}
}

func tcpState(hex string) string {
	switch strings.ToUpper(hex) {
	case "01":
		return "ESTABLISHED"
	case "02":
		return "SYN_SENT"
	case "03":
		return "SYN_RECV"
	case "04":
		return "FIN_WAIT1"
	case "05":
		return "FIN_WAIT2"
	case "06":
		return "TIME_WAIT"
	case "07":
		return "CLOSE"
	case "08":
		return "CLOSE_WAIT"
	case "09":
		return "LAST_ACK"
	case "0A":
		return "LISTEN"
	case "0B":
		return "CLOSING"
	default:
		return "OTHER"
	}
}

	if !ok || b != 255 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 255, got %d, ok=%v", b, ok)

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/aggregator.go
================================================================================
package pcap

import (
	"net/netip"
	"sync"
)

// IPRate — агрегат по одному IP за интервал.
type IPRate struct {
	SYN      uint64
	ACK      uint64
	RST      uint64
	FIN      uint64
	Bytes    uint64
	DstPorts map[uint16]struct{}
}

// Snapshot — агрегат за интервал.
//
// ВАЖНО: счётчики (SYN, ACK, RST, FIN, Bytes, PerIP) — абсолютные
// значения за окно между двумя последовательными Flush.
// Длительность окна задаётся вызывающим кодом (см. Pcap.AggInterval
// в конфиге). Детекторы в internal/detector/pkt.go интерпретируют
// свои пороги как «за окно», а не «per second». При AggInterval=1s
// это эквивалентно per-second.
type Snapshot struct {
	TotalPackets uint64
	Bytes        uint64
	SYN          uint64
	ACK          uint64
	RST          uint64
	FIN          uint64
	Dropped      uint64
	PerIP        map[netip.Addr]IPRate
}

type Aggregator struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	perIP map[netip.Addr]*IPRate
	total Snapshot
}

func NewAggregator() *Aggregator {
	return &Aggregator{perIP: make(map[netip.Addr]*IPRate)}
}

func (a *Aggregator) Observe(p PacketInfo) {
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()

	a.total.TotalPackets++
	a.total.Bytes += uint64(p.Length) // uint32 → uint64: безопасное расширение

	st := a.perIP[p.SrcIP]
	if st == nil {
		st = &IPRate{DstPorts: make(map[uint16]struct{})}
		a.perIP[p.SrcIP] = st
	}
	st.Bytes += uint64(p.Length)

	if p.Flags&tcpSYN != 0 {
		a.total.SYN++
		st.SYN++
	}
	if p.Flags&tcpACK != 0 {
		a.total.ACK++
		st.ACK++
	}
	if p.Flags&tcpRST != 0 {
		a.total.RST++
		st.RST++
	}
	if p.Flags&tcpFIN != 0 {
		a.total.FIN++
		st.FIN++
	}
	if p.DstPort != 0 {
		st.DstPorts[p.DstPort] = struct{}{}
	}
}

// Flush возвращает снимок за интервал и обнуляет состояние.
// dropped — значение Stats().Dropped, чтобы видеть переполнение канала.
func (a *Aggregator) Flush(dropped uint64) Snapshot {
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()

	out := Snapshot{
		TotalPackets: a.total.TotalPackets,
		Bytes:        a.total.Bytes,
		SYN:          a.total.SYN,
		ACK:          a.total.ACK,
		RST:          a.total.RST,
		FIN:          a.total.FIN,
		Dropped:      dropped,
		PerIP:        make(map[netip.Addr]IPRate, len(a.perIP)),
	}

	for ip, st := range a.perIP {
		out.PerIP[ip] = IPRate{
			SYN:      st.SYN,
			ACK:      st.ACK,
			RST:      st.RST,
			FIN:      st.FIN,
			Bytes:    st.Bytes,
			DstPorts: st.DstPorts,
		}
	}

	a.perIP = make(map[netip.Addr]*IPRate)
	a.total = Snapshot{}
	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/capture.go
================================================================================
package pcap

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
)

type Stats struct {
	TotalPackets uint64
	Bytes        uint64
	SYN          uint64
	ACK          uint64
	RST          uint64
	FIN          uint64
	Dropped      uint64
}

var (
	ErrNotSupported = errors.New("pcap: not supported on this platform")
	ErrNotPermitted = errors.New("pcap: permission denied (need CAP_NET_RAW or root)")
	ErrNoInterface  = errors.New("pcap: empty interface name")
	ErrAlreadyUp    = errors.New("pcap: capture already started")
)

type Capture interface {
	Start(ctx context.Context, iface string) error
	Stop() error
	Stats() Stats
	Packets() <-chan PacketInfo
}

// Stub — явная пустышка.
type Stub struct{}

func NewStub() Capture { return Stub{} }

func (Stub) Start(ctx context.Context, iface string) error { return ErrNotSupported }
func (Stub) Stop() error                                   { return nil }
func (Stub) Stats() Stats                                  { return Stats{} }

func (Stub) Packets() <-chan PacketInfo {
	ch := make(chan PacketInfo)
	close(ch) // consumer через `for p := range ch` сразу выйдет
	return ch
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/capture_linux.go
================================================================================
//go:build linux

package pcap

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"syscall"
	"unsafe"
)

const (
	readBufSize     = 65536
	defaultChanSize = 4096
	rcvbufSize      = 4 << 20
)

type statsCounters struct {
	totalPackets atomic.Uint64
	bytes        atomic.Uint64
	syn          atomic.Uint64
	ack          atomic.Uint64
	rst          atomic.Uint64
	fin          atomic.Uint64
	dropped      atomic.Uint64
}

type linuxCapture struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	fd      int
	iface   string
	cancel  context.CancelFunc
	wg      sync.WaitGroup
	stats   statsCounters
	packets chan PacketInfo

	// started — защита от повторного Start после Stop.
	// Stop закрывает c.packets, поэтому второй Start приведёт к
	// panic: send on closed channel. Капчур одноразовый.
	started bool
}

func New() Capture {
	return &linuxCapture{
		fd:      -1,
		packets: make(chan PacketInfo, defaultChanSize),
	}
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Start(ctx context.Context, iface string) error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()

	if c.started || c.fd >= 0 {
		return ErrAlreadyUp
	}
	if iface == "" {
		return ErrNoInterface
	}

	fd, err := syscall.Socket(
		syscall.AF_PACKET,
		syscall.SOCK_RAW,
		int(htons(syscall.ETH_P_ALL)),
	)
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, syscall.EPERM) || errors.Is(err, syscall.EACCES) {
			return ErrNotPermitted
		}
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: socket: %w", err)
	}

	_ = syscall.SetsockoptInt(fd, syscall.SOL_SOCKET, syscall.SO_RCVBUF, rcvbufSize)

	// BPF: принимаем только IPv4/TCP. IPv6 — задел на будущее.
	if err := attachTCPv4Filter(fd); err != nil {
		_ = syscall.Close(fd)
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: bpf: %w", err)
	}

	ifi, err := net.InterfaceByName(iface)
	if err != nil {
		_ = syscall.Close(fd)
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: interface %q: %w", iface, err)
	}

	sa := &syscall.SockaddrLinklayer{
		Protocol: htons(syscall.ETH_P_ALL),
		Ifindex:  ifi.Index,
	}
	if err := syscall.Bind(fd, sa); err != nil {
		_ = syscall.Close(fd)
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: bind %q: %w", iface, err)
	}

	tv := syscall.Timeval{Sec: 1}
	_ = syscall.SetsockoptTimeval(fd, syscall.SOL_SOCKET, syscall.SO_RCVTIMEO, &tv)

	rctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)

	c.fd = fd
	c.iface = iface
	c.cancel = cancel
	c.started = true

	c.wg.Add(1)
	go c.loop(rctx, fd)

	return nil
}

func (c *linuxCapture) loop(ctx context.Context, fd int) {
	defer c.wg.Done()

	buf := make([]byte, readBufSize)

	for {
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return
		}

		n, _, err := syscall.Recvfrom(fd, buf, 0)
		if err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, syscall.EAGAIN) ||
				errors.Is(err, syscall.EWOULDBLOCK) ||
				errors.Is(err, syscall.EINTR) {
				continue
			}
			return
		}
		if n <= 0 {
			continue
		}

		c.stats.totalPackets.Add(1)
		c.stats.bytes.Add(uint64(n))

		info, ok := parsePacket(buf[:n])
		if !ok {
			continue
		}

		if info.Flags&tcpSYN != 0 {
			c.stats.syn.Add(1)
		}
		if info.Flags&tcpACK != 0 {
			c.stats.ack.Add(1)
		}
		if info.Flags&tcpRST != 0 {
			c.stats.rst.Add(1)
		}
		if info.Flags&tcpFIN != 0 {
			c.stats.fin.Add(1)
		}

		// Не блокируем сниффер: канал переполнен — считаем drop.
		select {
		case c.packets <- info:
		default:
			c.stats.dropped.Add(1)
		}
	}
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Stop() error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	fd := c.fd
	cancel := c.cancel
	c.fd = -1
	c.cancel = nil
	c.mu.Unlock()

	if fd < 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if cancel != nil {
		cancel()
	}

	_ = syscall.Close(fd) // разблокирует Recvfrom
	c.wg.Wait()

	c.mu.Lock()
	close(c.packets)
	c.mu.Unlock()

	return nil
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Stats() Stats {
	return Stats{
		TotalPackets: c.stats.totalPackets.Load(),
		Bytes:        c.stats.bytes.Load(),
		SYN:          c.stats.syn.Load(),
		ACK:          c.stats.ack.Load(),
		RST:          c.stats.rst.Load(),
		FIN:          c.stats.fin.Load(),
		Dropped:      c.stats.dropped.Load(),
	}
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Packets() <-chan PacketInfo {
	return c.packets
}

// attachTCPv4Filter ставит классический BPF: ethertype==0x0800 && proto==6.
const tcpFilterLen uint16 = 6

func attachTCPv4Filter(fd int) error {
	filter := []syscall.SockFilter{
		{Code: 0x28, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 12},          // A = ethertype
		{Code: 0x15, Jt: 0, Jf: 3, K: ethTypeIPv4}, // if IPv4 → +2, else reject
		{Code: 0x30, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 23},          // A = IP proto
		{Code: 0x15, Jt: 1, Jf: 0, K: ipProtoTCP},  // if TCP → accept, else reject
		{Code: 0x06, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 0},           // reject
		{Code: 0x06, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 0xFFFFFFFF},  // accept
	}

	if len(filter) != int(tcpFilterLen) {
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: bpf filter length mismatch: got %d, want %d",
			len(filter), tcpFilterLen)
	}

	prog := syscall.SockFprog{
		Len:    tcpFilterLen, //#nosec G115 -- константа 6
		Filter: &filter[0],
	}

	// #nosec G103 -- unsafe.Pointer обязателен для SO_ATTACH_FILTER (BPF).
	_, _, errno := syscall.Syscall6(
		syscall.SYS_SETSOCKOPT,
		uintptr(fd),
		uintptr(syscall.SOL_SOCKET),
		uintptr(syscall.SO_ATTACH_FILTER),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&prog)),
		unsafe.Sizeof(prog),
		0,
	)
	if errno != 0 {
		return errno
	}

	return nil
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/parse.go
================================================================================
package pcap

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"net/netip"
	"time"
)

const (
	ethHdrLen   = 14
	vlanHdrLen  = 4
	ethTypeIPv4 = 0x0800
	ethTypeIPv6 = 0x86DD
	ethTypeVLAN = 0x8100
	ipProtoTCP  = 6

	tcpFIN = 0x01
	tcpSYN = 0x02
	tcpRST = 0x04
	tcpPSH = 0x08
	tcpACK = 0x10
	tcpURG = 0x20
)

// PacketInfo — разобранный TCP-пакет.
type PacketInfo struct {
	Time    time.Time
	SrcIP   netip.Addr
	DstIP   netip.Addr
	SrcPort uint16
	DstPort uint16
	Flags   uint8
	Proto   uint8
	Length  uint32
}

// parsePacket разбирает Ethernet-фрейм. ok=false — не TCP или битый.
func parsePacket(pkt []byte) (PacketInfo, bool) {
	if len(pkt) < ethHdrLen {
		return PacketInfo{}, false
	}

	etherType := binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[12:14])
	off := ethHdrLen

	if etherType == ethTypeVLAN {
		if len(pkt) < ethHdrLen+vlanHdrLen {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		etherType = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[16:18])
		off += vlanHdrLen
	}

	info := PacketInfo{Time: time.Now(), Length: uint32(len(pkt))} //#nosec G115 -- len(pkt) <= readBufSize (65536)

	switch etherType {
	case ethTypeIPv4:
		if len(pkt) < off+20 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		ihl := int(pkt[off]&0x0F) * 4
		if ihl < 20 || len(pkt) < off+ihl {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		proto := pkt[off+9]
		info.Proto = proto

		// Отбрасываем не-первые фрагменты.
		if binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[off+6:off+8])&0x1FFF != 0 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}

		src, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+12 : off+16])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		dst, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+16 : off+20])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcIP = src.Unmap()
		info.DstIP = dst.Unmap()

		if proto != ipProtoTCP {
			return info, true
		}

		tcpOff := off + ihl
		if len(pkt) < tcpOff+20 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff : tcpOff+2])
		info.DstPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff+2 : tcpOff+4])
		info.Flags = pkt[tcpOff+13]
		return info, true

	case ethTypeIPv6:
		if len(pkt) < off+40 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		proto := pkt[off+6]
		info.Proto = proto

		src, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+8 : off+24])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		dst, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+24 : off+40])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcIP = src.Unmap()
		info.DstIP = dst.Unmap()

		if proto != ipProtoTCP {
			return info, true
		}

		tcpOff := off + 40
		if len(pkt) < tcpOff+20 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff : tcpOff+2])
		info.DstPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff+2 : tcpOff+4])
		info.Flags = pkt[tcpOff+13]
		return info, true
	}

	return PacketInfo{}, false
}

func htons(v uint16) uint16 {
	return v<<8&0xFF00 | v>>8
}

package pipeline

import "alt-dosguard/internal/models"

func newAlertChannel(size int) chan models.Alert {
	if size <= 0 {
		size = 1024
	}

	return make(chan models.Alert, size)
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pipeline/pipeline.go
================================================================================
package pipeline

import (
	"context"
	"sync"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Pipeline struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	ch      chan models.Alert
	handler Handler
	wg      sync.WaitGroup
	stopped bool
}

func New(buffer int, handler Handler) *Pipeline {
	return &Pipeline{
		ch:      newAlertChannel(buffer),
		handler: handler,
	}
}

func (p *Pipeline) Start(ctx context.Context, workers int) {
	p.mu.Lock()
	if p.stopped {
		p.mu.Unlock()
		return
	}
	p.mu.Unlock()

	if workers <= 0 {
		workers = 1
	}

	for i := 0; i < workers; i++ {
		p.wg.Add(1)
		go runWorker(ctx, p.ch, p.handler, &p.wg)
	}
}

func (p *Pipeline) Submit(alert models.Alert) bool {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()

	if p.stopped {
		return false
	}

	select {
	case p.ch <- alert:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func (p *Pipeline) Stop() {
	p.mu.Lock()

	if !p.stopped {
		p.stopped = true
		close(p.ch)
	}

	p.mu.Unlock()

	p.wg.Wait()
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pipeline/workers.go
================================================================================
package pipeline

import (
	"context"
	"sync"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Handler func(ctx context.Context, alert models.Alert)

func runWorker(
	ctx context.Context,
	ch <-chan models.Alert,
	handler Handler,
	wg *sync.WaitGroup,
) {
	defer wg.Done()

	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case alert, ok := <-ch:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			if handler != nil {
				handler(ctx, alert)
			}
		}
	}
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/profiles/loader.go

package profiles

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"os"
)

func Load(path string) (*Engine, error) {
	if path == "" {
		return NewEngine(nil), nil
	}
    // #nosec G304 -- profile path from operator config
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read profiles: %w", err)
	}

	var f File

	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &f); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse profiles: %w", err)
	}

	return NewEngine(f.Profiles), nil
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/profiles/profiles.go
================================================================================
package profiles

import (
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Condition struct {
	Metric string  `json:"metric"`
	Op     string  `json:"op"`
	Value  float64 `json:"value"`
}

type Profile struct {
	ID          string      `json:"id"`
	Name        string      `json:"name"`
	Severity    string      `json:"severity"`
	Description string      `json:"description"`
	Conditions  []Condition `json:"conditions"`
}

type File struct {
	Profiles []Profile `json:"profiles"`
}

type Engine struct {
	profiles []Profile
}

func NewEngine(profiles []Profile) *Engine {
	return &Engine{profiles: profiles}
}

func (e *Engine) Evaluate(vars map[string]float64, now time.Time) []models.Alert {
	if e == nil || len(e.profiles) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	var out []models.Alert

	for _, p := range e.profiles {
		if !p.matches(vars) {
			continue
		}

		details := p.Description
		if details == "" {
			details = p.ID
		}

		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     now,
			IP:       "*",
			Type:     models.AlertType("PROFILE_" + p.ID),
			Severity: models.ParseSeverity(p.Severity),
			Details:  details,
		})
	}

	return out
}

func (p Profile) matches(vars map[string]float64) bool {
	if len(p.Conditions) == 0 {
		return false
	}

	for _, c := range p.Conditions {
		if !compare(vars[c.Metric], c.Op, c.Value) {
			return false
		}
	}

	return true
}

func compare(left float64, op string, right float64) bool {
	switch op {
	case ">":
		return left > right
	case ">=":
		return left >= right
	case "<":
		return left < right
	case "<=":
		return left <= right
	case "==":
		return left == right
	case "!=":
		return left != right
	default:
		return false
	}
}

package profiles

import "testing"

func TestProfile_Matches(t *testing.T) {
	p := Profile{
		Conditions: []Condition{{Metric: "x", Op: ">", Value: 10}},
	}
	if !p.matches(map[string]float64{"x": 20}) {
		t.Fatal("expected match")
	}
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/history.go
================================================================================
package state

import "sync"

type History struct {
	mu      sync.RWMutex
	data    []int
	maxSize int
}

func NewHistory(maxSize int) *History {
	if maxSize <= 0 {
		maxSize = 720
	}

	return &History{
		data:    make([]int, 0, maxSize),
		maxSize: maxSize,
	}
}

func (h *History) Add(value int) {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()

	h.data = append(h.data, value)

	if len(h.data) > h.maxSize {
		h.data = h.data[len(h.data)-h.maxSize:]
	}
}

func (h *History) AverageLast(n int) float64 {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	if n <= 0 || len(h.data) == 0 {
		return 0
	}

	if n > len(h.data) {
		n = len(h.data)
	}

	start := len(h.data) - n
	sum := 0

	for i := start; i < len(h.data); i++ {
		sum += h.data[i]
	}

	return float64(sum) / float64(n)
}

func (h *History) Current() int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	if len(h.data) == 0 {
		return 0
	}

	return h.data[len(h.data)-1]
}

func (h *History) Max() int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	maxValue := 0

	for _, v := range h.data {
		if v > maxValue {
			maxValue = v
		}
	}

	return maxValue
}

func (h *History) Len() int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	return len(h.data)
}

func (h *History) Values() []int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	out := make([]int, len(h.data))
	copy(out, h.data)

	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/ipstats.go
================================================================================
package state

import (
	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"sort"
)

// CopyIPStats возвращает глубокую копию карты IP-статистики.
func CopyIPStats(m map[string]*models.IPStat) map[string]*models.IPStat {
	if m == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := make(map[string]*models.IPStat, len(m))
	for k, v := range m {
		if v == nil {
			continue
		}
		cp := *v
		if v.Ports != nil {
			ports := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.Ports))
			for p := range v.Ports {
				ports[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.Ports = ports
		}
		if v.LocalPorts != nil {
			lp := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.LocalPorts))
			for p := range v.LocalPorts {
				lp[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.LocalPorts = lp
		}
		out[k] = &cp
	}
	return out
}

// TopIPs формирует отсортированный список TopIP.
func TopIPs(m map[string]*models.IPStat, limit int) []models.TopIP {
	out := make([]models.TopIP, 0, len(m))
	for _, st := range m {
		if st == nil {
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, models.TopIP{
			IP:        st.IP,
			Count:     st.IncomingConns, // топ по ВХОДЯЩИМ
			Ports:     len(st.LocalPorts),
			FirstSeen: st.FirstSeen,
			LastSeen:  st.LastSeen,
		})
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool {
		if out[i].Count != out[j].Count {
			return out[i].Count > out[j].Count
		}
		if out[i].Ports != out[j].Ports {
			return out[i].Ports > out[j].Ports
		}
		return out[i].IP < out[j].IP
	})
	if limit > 0 && len(out) > limit {
		out = out[:limit]
	}
	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/state_test.go

package state

import (
	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"testing"
)

func TestStore_Update(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewStore(Config{MaxConns: 100, MaxHistory: 10})
	conns := []*models.Connection{
		{LocalIP: "127.0.0.1", LocalPort: 80, RemoteIP: "1.1.1.1", RemotePort: 1234, State: "SYN_RECV", IsIncoming: true},
	}
	s.Update(conns)
	snap := s.GetSnapshot(10)
	if snap.TotalConns != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 conn, got %d", snap.TotalConns)
	}
	if snap.IncomingTotal != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 incoming, got %d", snap.IncomingTotal)
	}
}

func TestStore_OutgoingIgnored(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewStore(Config{MaxConns: 100, MaxHistory: 10})
	conns := []*models.Connection{
		{LocalIP: "192.168.1.10", LocalPort: 54321, RemoteIP: "8.8.8.8", RemotePort: 443, State: "ESTABLISHED", IsOutgoing: true},
	}
	s.Update(conns)
	snap := s.GetSnapshot(10)
	if snap.IncomingTotal != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 0 incoming, got %d", snap.IncomingTotal)
	}
	if snap.TotalConns != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 total, got %d", snap.TotalConns)
	}
}
func TestStore_IncomingOutgoing(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewStore(Config{MaxConns: 100, MaxHistory: 10})
	conns := []*models.Connection{
		{LocalIP: "192.168.1.10", LocalPort: 80, RemoteIP: "1.1.1.1", RemotePort: 54321, State: "ESTABLISHED", IsIncoming: true},
		{LocalIP: "192.168.1.10", LocalPort: 54321, RemoteIP: "8.8.8.8", RemotePort: 443, State: "ESTABLISHED", IsOutgoing: true},
	}
	s.Update(conns)
	snap := s.GetSnapshot(10)
	if snap.TotalConns != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 2 total, got %d", snap.TotalConns)
	}
	if snap.IncomingTotal != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 incoming, got %d", snap.IncomingTotal)
	}
	if snap.IncomingNew != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 incoming new, got %d", snap.IncomingNew)
	}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/store.go
================================================================================
package state

import (
	"sort"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Config struct {
	MaxConns       int
	MaxIPStats     int
	MaxHistory     int
	SmoothingAlpha float64
}

type Store struct {
	mu sync.RWMutex

	cfg Config

	connections map[string]*models.Connection
	ipStats     map[string]*models.IPStat
	stateStats  map[string]int

	history *History

	smoothedAvg   float64
	prevMovingAvg float64

	totalScans    int
	totalConns    int
	totalNew      int
	incomingTotal int
	incomingNew   int
}

type Snapshot struct {
	Time       time.Time
	TotalScans int
	TotalConns int
	TotalNew   int

	IncomingTotal int // сумма всех входящих соединений в newMap
	IncomingNew   int // новые входящие соединения за этот скан
	SmoothedAvg   float64
	MovingAvg     float64
	PrevMovingAvg float64

	StateStats map[string]int
	TopIPs     []models.TopIP
	IPStats    map[string]*models.IPStat
}

func NewStore(cfg Config) *Store {
	if cfg.MaxConns <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxConns = 10000
	}

	if cfg.MaxIPStats <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxIPStats = 10000
	}

	if cfg.MaxHistory <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxHistory = 720
	}

	if cfg.SmoothingAlpha <= 0 || cfg.SmoothingAlpha >= 1 {
		cfg.SmoothingAlpha = 0.3
	}

	return &Store{
		cfg:         cfg,
		connections: make(map[string]*models.Connection),
		ipStats:     make(map[string]*models.IPStat),
		stateStats:  make(map[string]int),
		history:     NewHistory(cfg.MaxHistory),
	}
}

func (s *Store) Update(conns []*models.Connection) {
	now := time.Now()

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	prevMoving := s.history.AverageLast(5)

	newMap := make(map[string]*models.Connection, len(conns))

	for _, c := range conns {
		if c == nil {
			continue
		}

		key := c.Key()

		old, exists := s.connections[key]
		if exists {
			c.FirstSeen = old.FirstSeen
			c.Count = old.Count + 1
		} else {
			c.FirstSeen = now
			c.Count = 1
		}

		c.LastSeen = now
		newMap[key] = c
	}

	if s.cfg.MaxConns > 0 && len(newMap) > s.cfg.MaxConns {
		pruneConnections(newMap, s.cfg.MaxConns)
	}

	newCount := 0
	incomingTotal := 0
	incomingNew := 0

	for key, c := range newMap {
		if c.IsIncoming {
			incomingTotal++
		}
		if _, ok := s.connections[key]; !ok {
			newCount++
			if c.IsIncoming {
				incomingNew++
			}
		}
	}

	stateStats := make(map[string]int)
	ipStats := make(map[string]*models.IPStat)

	for _, c := range newMap {
		stateStats[c.State]++

		st, ok := ipStats[c.RemoteIP]
		if !ok {
			st = &models.IPStat{
				IP:         c.RemoteIP,
				Ports:      make(map[int]struct{}),
				LocalPorts: make(map[int]struct{}),
				FirstSeen:  c.FirstSeen,
				LastSeen:   c.LastSeen,
			}
			ipStats[c.RemoteIP] = st
		}

		st.Count++

		// Направление трафика
		if c.IsIncoming {
			st.IncomingConns++
			st.LocalPorts[c.LocalPort] = struct{}{}
			if c.State == "SYN_RECV" {
				st.SynRecv++
				st.SynCount++
			}
		}
		if c.IsOutgoing {
			st.OutgoingConns++
			st.Ports[c.RemotePort] = struct{}{}
			if c.State == "SYN_SENT" {
				st.SynSent++
			}
		}

		// Fallback: если направление не определено (P2P), пишем в Ports
		if !c.IsIncoming && !c.IsOutgoing {
			st.Ports[c.RemotePort] = struct{}{}
		}

		if c.FirstSeen.Before(st.FirstSeen) {
			st.FirstSeen = c.FirstSeen
		}
		if c.LastSeen.After(st.LastSeen) {
			st.LastSeen = c.LastSeen
		}
	}

	if s.cfg.MaxIPStats > 0 && len(ipStats) > s.cfg.MaxIPStats {
		pruneIPStats(ipStats, s.cfg.MaxIPStats)
	}

	current := len(newMap)

	if s.totalScans == 0 {
		s.smoothedAvg = float64(current)
	} else {
		s.smoothedAvg = s.cfg.SmoothingAlpha*float64(current) +
			(1-s.cfg.SmoothingAlpha)*s.smoothedAvg
	}

	s.history.Add(current)

	s.connections = newMap
	s.ipStats = ipStats
	s.stateStats = stateStats
	s.totalConns = current
	s.totalNew = newCount
	s.incomingTotal = incomingTotal
	s.incomingNew = incomingNew
	s.prevMovingAvg = prevMoving
	s.totalScans++
}

func (s *Store) GetSnapshot(topLimit int) Snapshot {
	s.mu.RLock()
	defer s.mu.RUnlock()

	stateStats := make(map[string]int, len(s.stateStats))
	for k, v := range s.stateStats {
		stateStats[k] = v
	}

	// Глубокая копия: IPStat и вложенные мапы больше не шарятся со Store.
	ipStats := make(map[string]*models.IPStat, len(s.ipStats))
	for k, v := range s.ipStats {
		if v == nil {
			continue
		}
		cp := *v
		if v.Ports != nil {
			ports := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.Ports))
			for p := range v.Ports {
				ports[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.Ports = ports
		}
		if v.LocalPorts != nil {
			lp := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.LocalPorts))
			for p := range v.LocalPorts {
				lp[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.LocalPorts = lp
		}
		ipStats[k] = &cp
	}

	top := make([]models.TopIP, 0, len(ipStats))

	for _, st := range ipStats {
		top = append(top, models.TopIP{
			IP:        st.IP,
			Count:     st.Count,
			Ports:     len(st.Ports),
			FirstSeen: st.FirstSeen,
			LastSeen:  st.LastSeen,
		})
	}

	sort.Slice(top, func(i, j int) bool {
		if top[i].Count != top[j].Count {
			return top[i].Count > top[j].Count
		}

		if top[i].Ports != top[j].Ports {
			return top[i].Ports > top[j].Ports
		}

		return top[i].IP < top[j].IP
	})

	if topLimit > 0 && len(top) > topLimit {
		top = top[:topLimit]
	}

	return Snapshot{
		Time:          time.Now(),
		TotalScans:    s.totalScans,
		TotalConns:    s.totalConns,
		TotalNew:      s.totalNew,
		IncomingTotal: s.incomingTotal,
		IncomingNew:   s.incomingNew,
		SmoothedAvg:   s.smoothedAvg,
		MovingAvg:     s.history.AverageLast(5),
		PrevMovingAvg: s.prevMovingAvg,
		StateStats:    stateStats,
		TopIPs:        top,
		IPStats:       ipStats,
	}
}

func pruneConnections(m map[string]*models.Connection, max int) {
	if len(m) <= max {
		return
	}

	type item struct {
		key   string
		first time.Time
	}

	items := make([]item, 0, len(m))

	for k, v := range m {
		items = append(items, item{
			key:   k,
			first: v.FirstSeen,
		})
	}

	sort.Slice(items, func(i, j int) bool {
		if !items[i].first.Equal(items[j].first) {
			return items[i].first.After(items[j].first)
		}

		return items[i].key < items[j].key
	})

	for i := max; i < len(items); i++ {
		delete(m, items[i].key)
	}
}

func pruneIPStats(m map[string]*models.IPStat, max int) {
	if len(m) <= max {
		return
	}

	type item struct {
		ip    string
		count int
	}

	items := make([]item, 0, len(m))

	for ip, st := range m {
		items = append(items, item{
			ip:    ip,
			count: st.Count,
		})
	}

	sort.Slice(items, func(i, j int) bool {
		if items[i].count != items[j].count {
			return items[i].count > items[j].count
		}

		return items[i].ip < items[j].ip
	})

	for i := max; i < len(items); i++ {
		delete(m, items[i].ip)
	}
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/storage/enrichment_jsonl.go
================================================================================
package storage

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/threatintel"
)

// EnrichmentJSONLStore сохраняет результаты Threat Intelligence в JSONL.
type EnrichmentJSONLStore struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	path string
}

// NewEnrichmentJSONLStore создает хранилище результатов обогащения.
func NewEnrichmentJSONLStore(dir string) (*EnrichmentJSONLStore, error) {
	if dir == "" {
		dir = "data"
	}

	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &EnrichmentJSONLStore{
		path: filepath.Join(dir, "threatintel.jsonl"),
	}, nil
}

type enrichmentAlertRecord struct {
	Time     string `json:"time"`
	IP       string `json:"ip"`
	Type     string `json:"type"`
	Severity string `json:"severity"`
	Details  string `json:"details"`
}

type enrichmentRecord struct {
	Time    string                `json:"time"`
	Alert   enrichmentAlertRecord `json:"alert"`
	Cached  bool                  `json:"cached"`
	Skipped bool                  `json:"skipped"`
	Reason  string                `json:"reason,omitempty"`
	Error   string                `json:"error,omitempty"`
	Report  *threatintel.IPReport `json:"report,omitempty"`
}

// StoreEnrichment сохраняет один результат обогащения.
func (s *EnrichmentJSONLStore) StoreEnrichment(
	ctx context.Context,
	res threatintel.EnrichmentResult,
) error {
	if s == nil {
		return nil
	}

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.OpenFile(s.path, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()

	record := enrichmentRecord{
		Time: time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339),
		Alert: enrichmentAlertRecord{
			Time:     res.OriginalAlert.Time.Format(time.RFC3339),
			IP:       res.OriginalAlert.IP,
			Type:     string(res.OriginalAlert.Type),
			Severity: res.OriginalAlert.Severity.String(),
			Details:  res.OriginalAlert.Details,
		},
		Cached:  res.Cached,
		Skipped: res.Skipped,
		Reason:  res.Reason,
		Report:  res.Report,
	}

	if res.Err != nil {
		record.Error = res.Err.Error()
	}

	return json.NewEncoder(file).Encode(record)
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/storage/jsonl.go
================================================================================
package storage

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type AlertJSONLStore struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	path string
}

func NewAlertJSONLStore(dir string) (*AlertJSONLStore, error) {
	if dir == "" {
		dir = "data"
	}

	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &AlertJSONLStore{
		path: filepath.Join(dir, "alerts.jsonl"),
	}, nil
}

type alertRecord struct {
	Time     string `json:"time"`
	IP       string `json:"ip"`
	Type     string `json:"type"`
	Severity string `json:"severity"`
	Details  string `json:"details"`
}

func (s *AlertJSONLStore) AppendAlert(ctx context.Context, a models.Alert) (err error) {
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.OpenFile(s.path, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// Закрытие файла на записи: ошибка Close = возможная потеря данных,
	// поэтому не игнорируем, а возвращаем, если выше не было другой ошибки.
	defer func() {
		if cerr := file.Close(); cerr != nil && err == nil {
			err = fmt.Errorf("close jsonl file: %w", cerr)
		}
	}()

	rec := alertRecord{
		Time:     a.Time.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
		IP:       a.IP,
		Type:     string(a.Type),
		Severity: a.Severity.String(),
		Details:  a.Details,
	}

	return json.NewEncoder(file).Encode(rec)
}

func (s *AlertJSONLStore) QueryAlerts(ctx context.Context, limit int) ([]models.Alert, error) {
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.Open(s.path)
	if err != nil {
		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return nil, nil
		}
		return nil, err
	}

	// Чтение: ошибка Close не критична — закрываем и идём дальше.
	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()

	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 1024*1024), 1024*1024)

	var out []models.Alert

	for scanner.Scan() {
		var rec alertRecord

		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &rec); err != nil {
			continue
		}

		t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, rec.Time)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}

		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     t,
			IP:       rec.IP,
			Type:     models.AlertType(rec.Type),
			Severity: models.ParseSeverity(rec.Severity),
			Details:  rec.Details,
		})

		if limit > 0 && len(out) > limit {
			out = out[1:]
		}
	}

	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return out, nil
}

func (s *AlertJSONLStore) Close() error {
	return nil
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/abuseipdb.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"net/url"
	"time"
)

const (
	abuseIPDBBaseURL = "https://api.abuseipdb.com/api/v2/check"
	abuseIPDBMaxBody = 1 << 20 // 1 MiB
	abuseIPDBMaxAge  = 90      // дней истории
)

// AbuseIPDBClient — клиент AbuseIPDB API v2.
type AbuseIPDBClient struct {
	apiKey  string
	client  *http.Client
	limiter *TokenBucket
}

// NewAbuseIPDBClient создаёт клиент AbuseIPDB.
// limiter может быть nil — запросы идут без ограничения.
func NewAbuseIPDBClient(
	apiKey string,
	timeoutSec int,
	limiter *TokenBucket,
) *AbuseIPDBClient {
	return &AbuseIPDBClient{
		apiKey:  apiKey,
		client:  newHTTPClient(timeoutSec),
		limiter: limiter,
	}
}

func (c *AbuseIPDBClient) Name() string { return "abuseipdb" }

// Lookup выполняет запрос к AbuseIPDB.
func (c *AbuseIPDBClient) Lookup(ctx context.Context, ip string) (*IPReport, error) {
	if _, err := netip.ParseAddr(ip); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("abuseipdb: invalid ip %q: %w", ip, err)
	}

	if c.limiter != nil {
		if err := c.limiter.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("rate limiter aborted: %w", err)
		}
	}

	q := url.Values{}
	q.Set("ipAddress", ip)
	q.Set("maxAgeInDays", fmt.Sprintf("%d", abuseIPDBMaxAge))
	q.Set("verbose", "true")

	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(
		ctx,
		http.MethodGet,
		abuseIPDBBaseURL+"?"+q.Encode(),
		nil,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
	}

	req.Header.Set("Key", c.apiKey)
	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", userAgent)

	resp, err := c.client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("http request failed: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()

	switch resp.StatusCode {
	case http.StatusOK:
		return c.parseResponse(ip, io.LimitReader(resp.Body, abuseIPDBMaxBody))

	case http.StatusUnauthorized:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("abuseipdb: invalid API key (401)")

	case http.StatusUnprocessableEntity:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("abuseipdb: invalid ip %q (422)", ip)

	case http.StatusTooManyRequests:
		retryAfter := resp.Header.Get("Retry-After")
		if retryAfter != "" {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf(
				"abuseipdb: rate limit exceeded (429), retry-after=%s",
				retryAfter,
			)
		}
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("abuseipdb: rate limit exceeded (429)")

	default:
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 1024))
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"abuseipdb: api error %d: %s",
			resp.StatusCode,
			string(body),
		)
	}
}

func (c *AbuseIPDBClient) parseResponse(ip string, body io.Reader) (*IPReport, error) {
	var raw struct {
		Data struct {
			IPAddress            string `json:"ipAddress"`
			IsPublic             bool   `json:"isPublic"`
			IsWhitelisted        bool   `json:"isWhitelisted"`
			AbuseConfidenceScore int    `json:"abuseConfidenceScore"`
			CountryCode          string `json:"countryCode"`
			UsageType            string `json:"usageType"`
			ISP                  string `json:"isp"`
			Domain               string `json:"domain"`
			IsTor                bool   `json:"isTor"`
			TotalReports         int    `json:"totalReports"`
			NumDistinctUsers     int    `json:"numDistinctUsers"`
			LastReportedAt       string `json:"lastReportedAt"`
		} `json:"data"`
	}

	if err := json.NewDecoder(body).Decode(&raw); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("json decode error: %w", err)
	}

	d := raw.Data

	var lastReport time.Time
	if d.LastReportedAt != "" {
		if t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, d.LastReportedAt); err == nil {
			lastReport = t
		}
	}

	rep := &IPReport{
		IP:           ip,
		Provider:     c.Name(),
		Malicious:    d.AbuseConfidenceScore,
		Suspicious:   d.TotalReports,
		Undetected:   d.NumDistinctUsers,
		Reputation:   d.AbuseConfidenceScore,
		Country:      d.CountryCode,
		ASOwner:      d.ISP,
		LastAnalysis: lastReport,
		FetchedAt:    time.Now(),
	}

	if d.IsTor {
		if rep.Categories == nil {
			rep.Categories = map[string]string{}
		}
		rep.Categories["tor"] = "true"
	}
	if d.UsageType != "" {
		if rep.Categories == nil {
			rep.Categories = map[string]string{}
		}
		rep.Categories["usage_type"] = d.UsageType
	}
	if d.IsWhitelisted {
		if rep.Categories == nil {
			rep.Categories = map[string]string{}
		}
		rep.Categories["whitelisted"] = "true"
	}

	return rep, nil
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/cache.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"container/list"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// CacheItem — элемент кэша.
type CacheItem struct {
	Report    *IPReport
	ExpiresAt time.Time
}

type cacheEntry struct {
	key   string
	value *CacheItem
}

// LRUCache — потокобезопасный LRU-кэш с TTL.
type LRUCache struct {
	mu        sync.RWMutex
	items     map[string]*list.Element
	evictList *list.List
	maxSize   int
	ttl       time.Duration

	hits      uint64
	misses    uint64
	evictions uint64
}

// NewLRUCache создает кэш и запускает фоновую очистку протухших записей.
func NewLRUCache(maxSize int, ttlHours int) *LRUCache {
	if maxSize <= 0 {
		maxSize = 10000
	}

	ttl := time.Duration(ttlHours) * time.Hour
	if ttl <= 0 {
		ttl = 24 * time.Hour
	}

	c := &LRUCache{
		items:     make(map[string]*list.Element),
		evictList: list.New(),
		maxSize:   maxSize,
		ttl:       ttl,
	}

	go c.backgroundCleanup()

	return c
}

// Get возвращает отчет из кэша, если он существует и не протух.
func (c *LRUCache) Get(ip string) (*IPReport, bool) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()

	if ent, ok := c.items[ip]; ok {
		item := ent.Value.(*cacheEntry).value

		if time.Now().After(item.ExpiresAt) {
			c.removeElement(ent)
			c.misses++
			return nil, false
		}

		c.evictList.MoveToFront(ent)
		c.hits++
		return item.Report, true
	}

	c.misses++
	return nil, false
}

// Add добавляет или обновляет отчет в кэше.
func (c *LRUCache) Add(ip string, report *IPReport) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()

	if ent, ok := c.items[ip]; ok {
		c.evictList.MoveToFront(ent)
		ent.Value.(*cacheEntry).value = &CacheItem{
			Report:    report,
			ExpiresAt: time.Now().Add(c.ttl),
		}
		return
	}

	item := &CacheItem{
		Report:    report,
		ExpiresAt: time.Now().Add(c.ttl),
	}

	ent := c.evictList.PushFront(&cacheEntry{
		key:   ip,
		value: item,
	})

	c.items[ip] = ent

	if c.evictList.Len() > c.maxSize {
		c.removeOldest()
	}
}

// Stats возвращает статистику кэша.
func (c *LRUCache) Stats() (hits, misses, evictions, size uint64) {
	c.mu.RLock()
	defer c.mu.RUnlock()

	n := c.evictList.Len()
	if n < 0 {
		n = 0
	}

	return c.hits, c.misses, c.evictions, uint64(n)
}

func (c *LRUCache) removeOldest() {
	ent := c.evictList.Back()
	if ent != nil {
		c.removeElement(ent)
		c.evictions++
	}
}

func (c *LRUCache) removeElement(e *list.Element) {
	c.evictList.Remove(e)

	entry := e.Value.(*cacheEntry)
	delete(c.items, entry.key)
}

func (c *LRUCache) backgroundCleanup() {
	ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)
	defer ticker.Stop()

	for range ticker.C {
		c.mu.Lock()

		now := time.Now()
		var toRemove []*list.Element

		for e := c.evictList.Back(); e != nil; e = e.Prev() {
			item := e.Value.(*cacheEntry).value
			if now.After(item.ExpiresAt) {
				toRemove = append(toRemove, e)
			}
		}

		for _, e := range toRemove {
			c.removeElement(e)
		}

		c.mu.Unlock()
	}
}

FILE: internal/threatintel/cache_test.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestLRUCache_AddAndGet(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(10, 1)
	report := &IPReport{IP: "1.2.3.4", Malicious: 3}

	c.Add("1.2.3.4", report)

	got, ok := c.Get("1.2.3.4")
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("expected to find item in cache")
	}
	if got != report {
		t.Fatalf("expected same report pointer, got %#v", got)
	}

	// Несуществующий ключ.
	if _, ok := c.Get("9.9.9.9"); ok {
		t.Fatal("expected miss for unknown key")
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_Expired(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(10, 1)
	c.Add("1.2.3.4", &IPReport{IP: "1.2.3.4"})

	// TTL в конструкторе задаётся в часах, поэтому подменяем ExpiresAt
	// напрямую (тесты в том же пакете — white-box).
	c.mu.Lock()
	ent, ok := c.items["1.2.3.4"]
	if !ok {
		c.mu.Unlock()
		t.Fatal("item not found in internal map")
	}
	ent.Value.(*cacheEntry).value.ExpiresAt = time.Now().Add(-time.Second)
	c.mu.Unlock()

	if _, ok := c.Get("1.2.3.4"); ok {
		t.Fatal("expected expired entry to be a miss")
	}

	// Запись должна быть удалена из внутренней мапы.
	c.mu.RLock()
	_, exists := c.items["1.2.3.4"]
	c.mu.RUnlock()
	if exists {
		t.Fatal("expired entry should be deleted from internal map")
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_Eviction(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(2, 1)
	c.Add("a", &IPReport{IP: "a"})
	c.Add("b", &IPReport{IP: "b"})
	c.Add("c", &IPReport{IP: "c"}) // должен вытеснить "a"

	if _, ok := c.Get("a"); ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'a' to be evicted")
	}
	if _, ok := c.Get("b"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'b' to still be present")
	}
	if _, ok := c.Get("c"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'c' to still be present")
	}

	_, _, evictions, size := c.Stats()
	if evictions != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 eviction, got %d", evictions)
	}
	if size != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected size 2, got %d", size)
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_LRUOrder(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(2, 1)
	c.Add("a", &IPReport{IP: "a"})
	c.Add("b", &IPReport{IP: "b"})

	// "a" становится самой свежей после Get.
	if _, ok := c.Get("a"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'a'")
	}

	// При добавлении "c" должен вытесниться именно "b".
	c.Add("c", &IPReport{IP: "c"})

	if _, ok := c.Get("b"); ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'b' to be evicted (LRU)")
	}
	if _, ok := c.Get("a"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'a' to still be present")
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_Update(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(2, 1)
	c.Add("a", &IPReport{IP: "a", Malicious: 1})
	c.Add("a", &IPReport{IP: "a", Malicious: 5})

	got, ok := c.Get("a")
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("expected 'a'")
	}
	if got.Malicious != 5 {
		t.Fatalf("expected updated value 5, got %d", got.Malicious)
	}

	_, _, _, size := c.Stats()
	if size != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected size 1 after update, got %d", size)
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_Stats(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(10, 1)
	c.Add("a", &IPReport{IP: "a"})
	c.Add("b", &IPReport{IP: "b"})

	_, _ = c.Get("a") // hit
	_, _ = c.Get("b") // hit
	_, _ = c.Get("x") // miss
	_, _ = c.Get("y") // miss

	hits, misses, evictions, size := c.Stats()
	if hits != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 2 hits, got %d", hits)
	}
	if misses != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 2 misses, got %d", misses)
	}
	if evictions != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 0 evictions, got %d", evictions)
	}
	if size != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected size 2, got %d", size)
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_DefaultSize(t *testing.T) {
	// maxSize <= 0 → 10000.
	c := NewLRUCache(0, 1)
	if c.maxSize != 10000 {
		t.Fatalf("expected default maxSize 10000, got %d", c.maxSize)
	}
}

func TestLRUCache_ConcurrentAccess(t *testing.T) {
	c := NewLRUCache(50, 1)

	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 0; i < 200; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(n int) {
			defer wg.Done()
			key := string(rune('a' + n%26))
			c.Add(key, &IPReport{IP: key})
			_, _ = c.Get(key)
			_, _, _, _ = c.Stats()
		}(i)
	}
	wg.Wait()
}

FILE: internal/threatintel/client.go

package threatintel

import (
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

// userAgent — общий UA для всех HTTP-клиентов threatintel.
const userAgent = "alt-dosguard/1.0 (+threatintel)"

// newHTTPClient создаёт http.Client с таймаутом и запретом редиректов.
//
// Запрет редиректов — защита от SSRF: если внешний API вдруг ответит
// 30x на внутренний адрес, клиент не пойдёт по нему.
func newHTTPClient(timeoutSec int) *http.Client {
	if timeoutSec <= 0 {
		timeoutSec = 10
	}
	return &http.Client{
		Timeout: time.Duration(timeoutSec) * time.Second,
		CheckRedirect: func(req *http.Request, via []*http.Request) error {
			return http.ErrUseLastResponse
		},
	}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/knownbots.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"net"
	"strings"
)

// KnownBotFilter фильтрует известные CDN, облака и поисковые боты.
type KnownBotFilter struct {
	enabled  bool
	networks []*net.IPNet
	asnList  map[string]bool
}

// NewKnownBotFilter создает фильтр известных ботов и CDN.
func NewKnownBotFilter(enabled bool) *KnownBotFilter {
	f := &KnownBotFilter{
		enabled: enabled,
		asnList: make(map[string]bool),
	}

	if !enabled {
		return f
	}

	cidrs := []string{
		// Cloudflare
		"173.245.48.0/20",
		"103.21.244.0/22",
		"103.22.200.0/22",
		"103.31.4.0/22",
		"141.101.64.0/18",
		"108.162.192.0/18",
		"190.93.240.0/20",
		"188.114.96.0/20",
		"197.234.240.0/22",
		"198.41.128.0/17",
		"162.158.0.0/15",
		"104.16.0.0/13",
		"104.24.0.0/14",
		"172.64.0.0/13",
		"131.0.72.0/22",

		// Google
		"34.0.0.0/8",
		"35.0.0.0/8",
		"66.249.64.0/19",
		"64.233.160.0/19",

		// Amazon AWS
		"52.0.0.0/8",
		"54.0.0.0/8",
		"13.0.0.0/8",

		// Microsoft
		"20.0.0.0/8",
		"40.0.0.0/8",
		"157.55.39.0/24",
		"13.66.139.0/24",
	}

	for _, cidr := range cidrs {
		_, ipnet, err := net.ParseCIDR(cidr)
		if err == nil {
			f.networks = append(f.networks, ipnet)
		}
	}

	knownASNs := []string{
		"AS13335", // Cloudflare
		"AS15169", // Google
		"AS16509", // Amazon AWS
		"AS8075",  // Microsoft
		"AS32934", // Facebook
	}

	for _, asn := range knownASNs {
		f.asnList[asn] = true
	}

	return f
}

// IsKnownBot проверяет, входит ли IP в известные сети.
func (f *KnownBotFilter) IsKnownBot(ipStr string) bool {
	if !f.enabled {
		return false
	}

	ip := net.ParseIP(ipStr)
	if ip == nil {
		return false
	}

	for _, network := range f.networks {
		if network.Contains(ip) {
			return true
		}
	}

	return false
}

// IsKnownASN проверяет ASN.
func (f *KnownBotFilter) IsKnownASN(asn string) bool {
	if !f.enabled || asn == "" {
		return false
	}

	asn = strings.ToUpper(asn)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(asn, "AS") {
		asn = "AS" + asn
	}

	return f.asnList[asn]
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/ratelimit.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"context"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// TokenBucket — rate limiter на основе token bucket.
type TokenBucket struct {
	mu         sync.Mutex
	tokens     float64
	maxTokens  float64
	refillRate float64
	lastRefill time.Time
}

// NewRateLimiter создает лимитер для заданного числа запросов в минуту.
func NewRateLimiter(requestsPerMin int) *TokenBucket {
	if requestsPerMin <= 0 {
		requestsPerMin = 4
	}

	ratePerSec := float64(requestsPerMin) / 60.0

	return &TokenBucket{
		tokens:     float64(requestsPerMin),
		maxTokens:  float64(requestsPerMin),
		refillRate: ratePerSec,
		lastRefill: time.Now(),
	}
}

// Wait блокирует выполнение до появления токена или отмены контекста.
func (tb *TokenBucket) Wait(ctx context.Context) error {
	for {
		tb.mu.Lock()
		tb.refill()

		if tb.tokens >= 1.0 {
			tb.tokens -= 1.0
			tb.mu.Unlock()
			return nil
		}

		deficit := 1.0 - tb.tokens
		waitDuration := time.Duration(deficit / tb.refillRate * float64(time.Second))
		tb.mu.Unlock()

		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(waitDuration):
			// Повторно пробуем получить токен.
		}
	}
}

// refill добавляет токены в зависимости от прошедшего времени.
// Вызывать только под блокировкой.
func (tb *TokenBucket) refill() {
	now := time.Now()
	elapsed := now.Sub(tb.lastRefill).Seconds()

	tb.tokens += elapsed * tb.refillRate

	if tb.tokens > tb.maxTokens {
		tb.tokens = tb.maxTokens
	}

	tb.lastRefill = now
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/ratelimit_test.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestRateLimiter_AllowsBurst(t *testing.T) {
	rl := NewRateLimiter(60) // burst 60, refill 1/сек

	for i := 0; i < 60; i++ {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second)
		err := rl.Wait(ctx)
		cancel()
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("request %d: unexpected error: %v", i, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestRateLimiter_BlocksAfterLimit(t *testing.T) {
	rl := NewRateLimiter(60)

	// Исчерпываем burst.
	for i := 0; i < 60; i++ {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second)
		if err := rl.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
			cancel()
			t.Fatalf("request %d failed: %v", i, err)
		}
		cancel()
	}

	// Следующий запрос должен ждать ~1 сек; с коротким таймаутом
	// контекст отвалится раньше.
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 100*time.Millisecond)
	defer cancel()

	if err := rl.Wait(ctx); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected timeout, but Wait succeeded")
	}
}

func TestRateLimiter_ContextCancel(t *testing.T) {
	rl := NewRateLimiter(1) // burst 1, refill очень медленный

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second)
	if err := rl.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
		cancel()
		t.Fatalf("first request failed: %v", err)
	}
	cancel()

	// Второй запрос с уже отменённым контекстом.
	ctx, cancel = context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	cancel()

	err := rl.Wait(ctx)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected context.Canceled")
	}
	if err != context.Canceled {
		t.Fatalf("expected context.Canceled, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRateLimiter_ZeroRequestsPerMin(t *testing.T) {
	// requestsPerMin <= 0 → дефолт 4.
	rl := NewRateLimiter(0)
	if rl.maxTokens != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("expected default maxTokens=4, got %v", rl.maxTokens)
	}
	if rl.tokens != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("expected default tokens=4, got %v", rl.tokens)
	}
}

func TestRateLimiter_Refill(t *testing.T) {
	rl := NewRateLimiter(60) // refill 1 токен/сек

	// Исчерпываем burst.
	for i := 0; i < 60; i++ {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second)
		if err := rl.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
			cancel()
			t.Fatalf("request %d failed: %v", i, err)
		}
		cancel()
	}

	// Ждём пополнения.
	time.Sleep(1500 * time.Millisecond)

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 200*time.Millisecond)
	defer cancel()
	if err := rl.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("expected refilled token, got %v", err)
	}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/types.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

// Config — настройки модуля Threat Intelligence.
type Config struct {
	Enabled            bool   `yaml:"enabled"`
	Provider           string `yaml:"provider"`
	APIKey             string `yaml:"api_key"`
	CacheTTLHours      int    `yaml:"cache_ttl_hours"`
	CacheMaxSize       int    `yaml:"cache_max_size"`
	MaxRequestsPerMin  int    `yaml:"max_requests_per_min"`
	MinSeverityToCheck string `yaml:"min_severity_to_check"`
	IgnoreKnownBots    bool   `yaml:"ignore_known_bots"`
	TimeoutSec         int    `yaml:"timeout_sec"`
	EnrichStorage      bool   `yaml:"enrich_storage"`
	Workers            int    `yaml:"workers"`
}

// DefaultConfig возвращает настройки Threat Intelligence по умолчанию.
//
// APIKey намеренно пустой: секрет задаётся через переменную окружения
// VIRUSTOTAL_KEY и подставляется на этапе загрузки конфигурации
// (см. config loader / env-expansion), а не в DefaultConfig.
func DefaultConfig() Config {
	return Config{
		Enabled:            false,
		Provider:           "virustotal",
		APIKey:             "",
		CacheTTLHours:      48,
		CacheMaxSize:       10000,
		MaxRequestsPerMin:  4,
		MinSeverityToCheck: "MED",
		IgnoreKnownBots:    true,
		TimeoutSec:         10,
		EnrichStorage:      true,
	}
}

// IPReport — отчет по IP от внешнего TI-провайдера.
type IPReport struct {
	IP           string
	Provider     string
	Malicious    int
	Suspicious   int
	Harmless     int
	Undetected   int
	Reputation   int
	Categories   map[string]string
	Country      string
	ASN          int
	ASOwner      string
	LastAnalysis time.Time
	FetchedAt    time.Time
}

// Provider — интерфейс внешнего TI-провайдера.
type Provider interface {
	Name() string
	Lookup(ctx context.Context, ip string) (*IPReport, error)
}

// EnrichmentResult — результат обогащения алерта.
type EnrichmentResult struct {
	OriginalAlert models.Alert
	Report        *IPReport
	Err           error
	Cached        bool
	Skipped       bool
	Reason        string
}

// EnrichmentSink — интерфейс хранилища результатов обогащения.
type EnrichmentSink interface {
	StoreEnrichment(ctx context.Context, result EnrichmentResult) error
}

// Logger — минимальный интерфейс логгера для threatintel.
type Logger interface {
	Info(msg string)
	Warn(msg string)
	Error(msg string)
}

// SeverityThreshold — маппинг строковых уровней в models.Severity.
var SeverityThreshold = map[string]models.Severity{
	"INFO": models.SeverityInfo,
	"LOW":  models.SeverityLow,
	"MED":  models.SeverityMed,
	"HIGH": models.SeverityHigh,
	"CRIT": models.SeverityCrit,
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/vt.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"time"
)

const (
	vtBaseURL     = "https://www.virustotal.com/api/v3/ip_addresses/%s"
	vtMaxBodySize = 1 << 20 // 1 MiB — с запасом на JSON VT
)

// VirusTotalClient — клиент VirusTotal API v3.
type VirusTotalClient struct {
	apiKey  string
	client  *http.Client
	limiter *TokenBucket
}

// NewVirusTotalClient создаёт клиент VirusTotal.
// limiter может быть nil — тогда запросы идут без ограничения.
func NewVirusTotalClient(
	apiKey string,
	timeoutSec int,
	limiter *TokenBucket,
) *VirusTotalClient {
	return &VirusTotalClient{
		apiKey:  apiKey,
		client:  newHTTPClient(timeoutSec),
		limiter: limiter,
	}
}

func (v *VirusTotalClient) Name() string {
	return "virustotal"
}

// Lookup выполняет запрос к VirusTotal.
//
// Поведение при 404: VT не знает этот IP — это НЕ ошибка, возвращается
// пустой IPReport с заполненными IP/Provider/FetchedAt.
func (v *VirusTotalClient) Lookup(ctx context.Context, ip string) (*IPReport, error) {
	// Валидируем IP до запроса: PathEscape не спасает от мусора,
	// а VT на кривой ввод вернёт 404, который мы бы вернули как «нет данных».
	if _, err := netip.ParseAddr(ip); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("virustotal: invalid ip %q: %w", ip, err)
	}

	if v.limiter != nil {
		if err := v.limiter.Wait(ctx); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("rate limiter aborted: %w", err)
		}
	}

	urlStr := fmt.Sprintf(vtBaseURL, ip)

	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, urlStr, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
	}

	req.Header.Set("x-apikey", v.apiKey)
	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", userAgent)

	resp, err := v.client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("http request failed: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()

	switch resp.StatusCode {
	case http.StatusOK:
		return v.parseResponse(ip, io.LimitReader(resp.Body, vtMaxBodySize))

	case http.StatusNotFound:
		return &IPReport{
			IP:        ip,
			Provider:  v.Name(),
			FetchedAt: time.Now(),
		}, nil

	case http.StatusUnauthorized:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("virustotal: invalid API key (401)")

	case http.StatusTooManyRequests:
		retryAfter := resp.Header.Get("Retry-After")
		if retryAfter != "" {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf(
				"virustotal API rate limit exceeded (429), retry-after=%s",
				retryAfter,
			)
		}
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("virustotal API rate limit exceeded (429)")

	default:
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 1024))
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"virustotal API error %d: %s",
			resp.StatusCode,
			string(body),
		)
	}
}

func (v *VirusTotalClient) parseResponse(ip string, body io.Reader) (*IPReport, error) {
	var vtResp struct {
		Data struct {
			Attributes struct {
				LastAnalysisStats struct {
					Malicious  int `json:"malicious"`
					Suspicious int `json:"suspicious"`
					Harmless   int `json:"harmless"`
					Undetected int `json:"undetected"`
				} `json:"last_analysis_stats"`

				Reputation   int               `json:"reputation"`
				Country      string            `json:"country"`
				ASN          uint32            `json:"asn"`
				ASOwner      string            `json:"as_owner"`
				LastAnalysis int64             `json:"last_analysis_date"`
				Categories   map[string]string `json:"categories"`
			} `json:"attributes"`
		} `json:"data"`
	}

	if err := json.NewDecoder(body).Decode(&vtResp); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("json decode error: %w", err)
	}

	attrs := vtResp.Data.Attributes

	var lastAnalysisTime time.Time
	if attrs.LastAnalysis > 0 {
		lastAnalysisTime = time.Unix(attrs.LastAnalysis, 0)
	}

	// IPReport.ASN — int. На 64-битных платформах сужение безопасно.
	// ASN реально укладываются в 32 бита (макс 4294967295).
	return &IPReport{
		IP:           ip,
		Provider:     v.Name(),
		Malicious:    attrs.LastAnalysisStats.Malicious,
		Suspicious:   attrs.LastAnalysisStats.Suspicious,
		Harmless:     attrs.LastAnalysisStats.Harmless,
		Undetected:   attrs.LastAnalysisStats.Undetected,
		Reputation:   attrs.Reputation,
		Country:      attrs.Country,
		ASN:          int(attrs.ASN),
		ASOwner:      attrs.ASOwner,
		Categories:   attrs.Categories,
		LastAnalysis: lastAnalysisTime,
		FetchedAt:    time.Now(),
	}, nil
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/threatintel/worker.go
================================================================================
package threatintel

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"strings"
	"sync"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type nopLogger struct{}

func (nopLogger) Info(string)  {}
func (nopLogger) Warn(string)  {}
func (nopLogger) Error(string) {}

// Worker — асинхронный воркер обогащения алертов.
type Worker struct {
	cfg      Config
	provider Provider
	cache    *LRUCache
	bots     *KnownBotFilter
	logger   Logger
	sink     EnrichmentSink

	inChan chan models.Alert

	mu      sync.RWMutex
	stopped bool
	started bool

	stopOnce  sync.Once
	startOnce sync.Once
	wg        sync.WaitGroup
}

// NewWorker создаёт воркер Threat Intelligence.
//
// Если cfg.Enabled == false, возвращает (nil, nil) — это соглашение
// используется вызывающим кодом (app.go) для отключения подсистемы.
func NewWorker(cfg Config, logger Logger, sink EnrichmentSink) (*Worker, error) {
	if !cfg.Enabled {
		return nil, nil
	}

	if logger == nil {
		logger = nopLogger{}
	}

	if invalidAPIKey(cfg.APIKey) {
		envVar := "VIRUSTOTAL_KEY"
		if strings.EqualFold(cfg.Provider, "abuseipdb") {
			envVar = "ABUSEIPDB_KEY"
		}
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"threat_intel.api_key is missing or placeholder: "+
				"задайте переменную %s или укажите ключ в конфиге",
			envVar,
		)
	}

	limiter := NewRateLimiter(cfg.MaxRequestsPerMin)
	cache := NewLRUCache(cfg.CacheMaxSize, cfg.CacheTTLHours)
	bots := NewKnownBotFilter(cfg.IgnoreKnownBots)

	var provider Provider

	switch strings.ToLower(cfg.Provider) {
	case "virustotal":
		provider = NewVirusTotalClient(cfg.APIKey, cfg.TimeoutSec, limiter)
	case "abuseipdb":
		provider = NewAbuseIPDBClient(cfg.APIKey, cfg.TimeoutSec, limiter)
	default:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"unsupported threat intel provider: %q (supported: virustotal, abuseipdb)",
			cfg.Provider,
		)
	}

	return &Worker{
		cfg:      cfg,
		provider: provider,
		cache:    cache,
		bots:     bots,
		logger:   logger,
		sink:     sink,
		inChan:   make(chan models.Alert, 1000),
	}, nil
}

// Submit отправляет алерт в очередь обогащения.
// Не блокирует вызывающую горутину.
func (w *Worker) Submit(alert models.Alert) {
	if w == nil {
		return
	}

	// TI обогащает только конкретные IP. "*" — маркер глобального алерта
	// (профили, churn, spike), у него нет владельца для lookup.
	if net.ParseIP(alert.IP) == nil {
		return
	}

	w.mu.RLock()
	defer w.mu.RUnlock()

	if w.stopped {
		return
	}

	select {
	case w.inChan <- alert:
	default:
		w.logger.Warn(fmt.Sprintf(
			"ThreatIntel queue full, dropping enrichment for %s",
			alert.IP,
		))
	}
}

// Start запускает фоновые горутины обработки.
// Повторный вызов после Stop или второй Start — no-op.
func (w *Worker) Start(ctx context.Context, numWorkers int) {
	if w == nil {
		return
	}

	if numWorkers <= 0 {
		numWorkers = 1
	}

	w.mu.Lock()
	if w.stopped || w.started {
		w.mu.Unlock()
		return
	}
	w.started = true
	w.mu.Unlock()

	w.startOnce.Do(func() {
		for i := 0; i < numWorkers; i++ {
			w.wg.Add(1)
			go w.loop(ctx, i)
		}
	})

	w.logger.Info(fmt.Sprintf(
		"ThreatIntel Worker started: workers=%d, provider=%s",
		numWorkers,
		w.cfg.Provider,
	))
}

// Stop корректно останавливает воркер. Идемпотентен.
func (w *Worker) Stop() {
	if w == nil {
		return
	}

	w.stopOnce.Do(func() {
		w.mu.Lock()
		w.stopped = true
		close(w.inChan)
		w.mu.Unlock()

		w.wg.Wait()
	})
}

func (w *Worker) loop(ctx context.Context, id int) {
	defer w.wg.Done()

	minSeverity, ok := SeverityThreshold[strings.ToUpper(w.cfg.MinSeverityToCheck)]
	if !ok {
		minSeverity = models.SeverityMed
	}

	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case alert, ok := <-w.inChan:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			w.handle(ctx, alert, minSeverity)
		}
	}
}

func (w *Worker) handle(ctx context.Context, alert models.Alert, minSeverity models.Severity) {
	result := EnrichmentResult{
		OriginalAlert: alert,
	}

	// 1. Фильтр по минимальному уровню серьёзности.
	if alert.Severity < minSeverity {
		result.Skipped = true
		result.Reason = "severity_below_threshold"
		w.processResult(ctx, result)
		return
	}

	// 2. Фильтр известных ботов и CDN.
	if w.bots.IsKnownBot(alert.IP) {
		result.Skipped = true
		result.Reason = "known_bot_or_cdn"
		w.processResult(ctx, result)
		return
	}

	// 3. Проверка локального кэша.
	if report, ok := w.cache.Get(alert.IP); ok {
		result.Report = report
		result.Cached = true
		w.processResult(ctx, result)
		return
	}

	// 4. Запрос к внешнему API.
	report, err := w.provider.Lookup(ctx, alert.IP)
	if err != nil {
		result.Err = err
		w.logger.Warn(fmt.Sprintf(
			"TI lookup failed for %s: %v",
			alert.IP,
			err,
		))
		w.processResult(ctx, result)
		return
	}

	// 5. Дополнительная проверка по ASN.
	if w.bots.IsKnownASN(fmt.Sprintf("AS%d", report.ASN)) {
		result.Skipped = true
		result.Reason = "known_asn"
		w.processResult(ctx, result)
		return
	}

	w.cache.Add(alert.IP, report)
	result.Report = report
	w.processResult(ctx, result)
}

func (w *Worker) processResult(ctx context.Context, res EnrichmentResult) {
	if ctx.Err() != nil {
		return
	}

	if w.sink != nil {
		if err := w.sink.StoreEnrichment(ctx, res); err != nil {
			w.logger.Error(fmt.Sprintf(
				"Failed to store enrichment result: %v",
				err,
			))
		}
	}

	if res.Report != nil && res.Report.Malicious > 0 {
		w.logger.Info(fmt.Sprintf(
			"[THREAT_INTEL] IP %s flagged as MALICIOUS: detections=%d, provider=%s, owner=%s, country=%s",
			res.OriginalAlert.IP,
			res.Report.Malicious,
			res.Report.Provider,
			res.Report.ASOwner,
			res.Report.Country,
		))
	}
}

func invalidAPIKey(s string) bool {
	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
	if s == "" {
		return true
	}

	// Явные placeholder'ы, которые встречаются в шаблонах.
	if strings.HasPrefix(s, "${") && strings.HasSuffix(s, "}") {
		return true
	}

	lower := strings.ToLower(s)

	placeholders := []string{
		"ваш_ключ",
		"your_api_key",
		"your_key",
		"change_me",
		"<your_api_key>",
		"<api_key>",
	}

	for _, p := range placeholders {
		if strings.Contains(lower, p) {
			return true
		}
	}

	return false
}

FILE: internal/threatintel/worker_test.go
