package detector

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/alerts"
	"alt-dosguard/internal/state"
)

func TestDetector_Warmup(t *testing.T) {
	am := alerts.New(10, time.Second)
	d := New(Config{WarmupScans: 3, ConnThreshold: 10}, am)
	snap := state.Snapshot{TotalScans: 1, TotalConns: 100}
	d.Detect(snap)
	if am.Total() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("expected no alerts during warmup")
	}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/detector/pkt.go
================================================================================
package detector

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/netip"
	"sort"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"alt-dosguard/internal/pcap"
)

const defaultMaxPktAlertsPerScan = 50

// DetectSYNFloodByPPS — глобальный SYN-флуд по pps; низкий ACK-ratio = спуфинг.
func DetectSYNFloodByPPS(p pcap.Snapshot, synThreshold uint64, minACKRatio float64) []models.Alert {
	if synThreshold == 0 || p.SYN < synThreshold {
		return nil
	}

	ackRatio := 0.0
	if p.SYN > 0 {
		ackRatio = float64(p.ACK) / float64(p.SYN)
	}

	sev := models.SeverityHigh
	if minACKRatio > 0 && ackRatio < minACKRatio {
		sev = models.SeverityCrit
	}

	return []models.Alert{{
		Time:     time.Now(),
		IP:       "*",
		Type:     models.AlertSYNFlood,
		Severity: sev,
		Details: fmt.Sprintf(
			"SYN-flood (pcap): SYN/s=%d ACK/s=%d ratio=%.2f (порог SYN/s=%d, minACK=%.2f)",
			p.SYN, p.ACK, ackRatio, synThreshold, minACKRatio,
		),
	}}
}

// DetectPerIPSYNRate — IP с высоким SYN/s.
func DetectPerIPSYNRate(p pcap.Snapshot, threshold uint64, maxAlerts int) []models.Alert {
	if threshold == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if maxAlerts <= 0 {
		maxAlerts = defaultMaxPktAlertsPerScan
	}

	type hit struct {
		ip  netip.Addr
		syn uint64
	}
	var hits []hit

	for ip, st := range p.PerIP {
		if st.SYN >= threshold {
			hits = append(hits, hit{ip: ip, syn: st.SYN})
		}
	}
	if len(hits) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	sort.Slice(hits, func(i, j int) bool {
		if hits[i].syn != hits[j].syn {
			return hits[i].syn > hits[j].syn
		}
		return hits[i].ip.Less(hits[j].ip)
	})
	if len(hits) > maxAlerts {
		hits = hits[:maxAlerts]
	}

	now := time.Now()
	out := make([]models.Alert, 0, len(hits))
	for _, h := range hits {
		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     now,
			IP:       h.ip.String(),
			Type:     models.AlertSYNFlood,
			Severity: models.SeverityHigh,
			Details: fmt.Sprintf(
				"SYN/s с %s: %d (порог %d)", h.ip, h.syn, threshold,
			),
		})
	}
	return out
}

// DetectRSTScan — RST-скан: много RST/s и много уникальных dst-портов с одного IP.
func DetectRSTScan(p pcap.Snapshot, minRST uint64, minPorts, maxAlerts int) []models.Alert {
	if minRST == 0 || minPorts <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if maxAlerts <= 0 {
		maxAlerts = defaultMaxPktAlertsPerScan
	}

	type hit struct {
		ip    netip.Addr
		rst   uint64
		ports int
	}
	var hits []hit

	for ip, st := range p.PerIP {
		if st.RST < minRST {
			continue
		}
		if len(st.DstPorts) < minPorts {
			continue
		}
		hits = append(hits, hit{ip: ip, rst: st.RST, ports: len(st.DstPorts)})
	}
	if len(hits) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	sort.Slice(hits, func(i, j int) bool {
		if hits[i].ports != hits[j].ports {
			return hits[i].ports > hits[j].ports
		}
		if hits[i].rst != hits[j].rst {
			return hits[i].rst > hits[j].rst
		}
		return hits[i].ip.Less(hits[j].ip)
	})
	if len(hits) > maxAlerts {
		hits = hits[:maxAlerts]
	}

	now := time.Now()
	out := make([]models.Alert, 0, len(hits))
	for _, h := range hits {
		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     now,
			IP:       h.ip.String(),
			Type:     models.AlertPortScan,
			Severity: models.SeverityHigh,
			Details: fmt.Sprintf(
				"RST-скан (pcap): %s RST/s=%d портов=%d (порог RST/s=%d, ports=%d)",
				h.ip, h.rst, h.ports, minRST, minPorts,
			),
		})
	}
	return out
}

// DetectByteFlood — объёмный флуд по байтам.
func DetectByteFlood(p pcap.Snapshot, maxBytes uint64) []models.Alert {
	if maxBytes == 0 || p.Bytes < maxBytes {
		return nil
	}
	return []models.Alert{{
		Time:     time.Now(),
		IP:       "*",
		Type:     models.AlertTrafficSpike,
		Severity: models.SeverityHigh,
		Details: fmt.Sprintf(
			"Объёмный флуд (pcap): %d байт/сек (порог %d)", p.Bytes, maxBytes,
		),
	}}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/detector/portscan.go
================================================================================
package detector

import (
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"alt-dosguard/internal/state"
)

// DetectPortScan ищет IP, у которых число различных удалённых портов >= ports.
// Лимит алертов за скан — 20 (см. DetectPortScanWithLimit).
func DetectPortScan(s state.Snapshot, ports int) []models.Alert {
	return DetectPortScanWithLimit(s, ports, 20)
}

// DetectPortScanWithLimit — вариант с явным лимитом алертов и сортировкой:
// сначала IP с наибольшим числом портов, при равенстве — по IP.
func DetectPortScanWithLimit(s state.Snapshot, ports, maxAlerts int) []models.Alert {
	if ports <= 0 {
		return nil
	}

	if maxAlerts <= 0 {
		maxAlerts = 20
	}

	type hit struct {
		ip    string
		ports int
	}

	var hits []hit

	for ip, st := range s.IPStats {
		if st == nil {
			continue
		}
		// Реальный порт-скан: атакующий стучится на разные НАШИ порты.
		// Считаем уникальные локальные порты, а не удалённые.
		// Если LocalPorts пустой — соединение не было входящим или парсер
		// не определил направление; такой хит не является сканом.
		n := 0
		if st.LocalPorts != nil {
			n = len(st.LocalPorts)
		}
		if n < ports {
			continue
		}
		hits = append(hits, hit{ip: ip, ports: n})
	}

	if len(hits) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	sort.Slice(hits, func(i, j int) bool {
		if hits[i].ports != hits[j].ports {
			return hits[i].ports > hits[j].ports
		}

		return hits[i].ip < hits[j].ip
	})

	limit := maxAlerts
	if len(hits) < limit {
		limit = len(hits)
	}

	now := time.Now()
	out := make([]models.Alert, 0, limit)

	for i := 0; i < limit; i++ {
		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     now,
			IP:       hits[i].ip,
			Type:     models.AlertPortScan,
			Severity: models.SeverityHigh,
			Details: fmt.Sprintf(
				"Возможное сканирование портов: %s, уникальных локальных портов: %d (порог: %d)",
				hits[i].ip,
				hits[i].ports,
				ports,
			),
		})
	}

	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/detector/profile.go
================================================================================
package detector

import (
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/profiles"
	"alt-dosguard/internal/state"
)

// buildProfileVars — маппинг state.Snapshot → map[string]float64 для profiles.Engine.
//
// Все метрики ориентированы на ВХОДЯЩИЙ трафик, чтобы поведенческие
// профили не срабатывали на исходящие подключения браузера к CDN.
func buildProfileVars(s state.Snapshot) map[string]float64 {
	synRecv := float64(s.StateStats["SYN_RECV"])
	incoming := float64(s.IncomingTotal)

	vars := map[string]float64{
		"total_conns":    float64(s.TotalConns),
		"incoming_conns": incoming,
		"total_new":      float64(s.TotalNew),
		"syn_recv":       synRecv,
		"syn_sent":       float64(s.StateStats["SYN_SENT"]),
		"established":    float64(s.StateStats["ESTABLISHED"]),
	}

	if incoming > 0 {
		vars["syn_ratio"] = synRecv / incoming
	} else if synRecv > 0 {
		vars["syn_ratio"] = 1.0
	}

	var topIncoming, topLocalPorts int
	for _, st := range s.IPStats {
		if st == nil {
			continue
		}
		if st.IncomingConns > topIncoming {
			topIncoming = st.IncomingConns
		}
		if st.LocalPorts != nil && len(st.LocalPorts) > topLocalPorts {
			topLocalPorts = len(st.LocalPorts)
		}
	}

	vars["top_ip_incoming_conns"] = float64(topIncoming)
	vars["top_ip_local_ports"] = float64(topLocalPorts)

	if incoming > 0 {
		vars["top_ip_incoming_ratio"] = float64(topIncoming) / incoming
	}

	return vars
}

// DetectProfiles прогоняет snapshot через профили и заводит совпавшие алерты
// тем же путём d.add, что и /proc-детекторы: дедуп + onAlert.
func (d *Detector) DetectProfiles(engine *profiles.Engine, s state.Snapshot) {
	if d == nil || d.alerts == nil || engine == nil {
		return
	}
	if s.TotalScans <= d.cfg.WarmupScans {
		return
	}
	for _, a := range engine.Evaluate(buildProfileVars(s), time.Now()) {
		d.add(a)
	}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/detector/slowloris.go
================================================================================
package detector

import (
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"alt-dosguard/internal/state"
)

const defaultMaxSlowlorisAlerts = 20

// DetectSlowloris ищет медленную DoS-атаку (Slowloris / R-U-Dead-Yet).
//
// Признак: один IP держит много ВХОДЯЩИХ соединений,
// при этом все они давно установлены (не закрываются),
// но при этом не передают данных. В /proc это выглядит как
// много входящих соединений с большим временем жизни.
//
// minConns  — минимум входящих соединений от одного IP.
// minAge    — минимальный возраст самого старого соединения.
// maxAlerts — лимит алертов за скан.
func DetectSlowloris(s state.Snapshot, minConns int, minAge time.Duration) []models.Alert {
	return DetectSlowlorisWithLimit(s, minConns, minAge, defaultMaxSlowlorisAlerts)
}

func DetectSlowlorisWithLimit(
	s state.Snapshot,
	minConns int,
	minAge time.Duration,
	maxAlerts int,
) []models.Alert {
	if minConns <= 0 {
		minConns = 50
	}
	if minAge <= 0 {
		minAge = 5 * time.Minute
	}
	if maxAlerts <= 0 {
		maxAlerts = defaultMaxSlowlorisAlerts
	}

	now := time.Now()

	type hit struct {
		ip     string
		conns  int
		oldest time.Time
	}
	var hits []hit

	for ip, st := range s.IPStats {
		if st == nil {
			continue
		}
		// Только входящие соединения (кто-то держит их на нас).
		if st.IncomingConns < minConns {
			continue
		}
		// Если исходящих больше чем входящих — это мы к нему ходим, не атака.
		if st.OutgoingConns > st.IncomingConns {
			continue
		}
		// FirstSeen не заполнен — возраст недостоверен, пропускаем.
		// Без этой проверки now.Sub(time.Time{}) ≈ 55 лет и хит пройдёт всегда.
		if st.FirstSeen.IsZero() {
			continue
		}
		// Самое старое соединение должно быть старше minAge.
		age := now.Sub(st.FirstSeen)
		if age < minAge {
			continue
		}
		hits = append(hits, hit{
			ip:     ip,
			conns:  st.IncomingConns,
			oldest: st.FirstSeen,
		})
	}

	if len(hits) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	sort.Slice(hits, func(i, j int) bool {
		if hits[i].conns != hits[j].conns {
			return hits[i].conns > hits[j].conns
		}
		return hits[i].ip < hits[j].ip
	})

	if len(hits) > maxAlerts {
		hits = hits[:maxAlerts]
	}

	out := make([]models.Alert, 0, len(hits))
	for _, h := range hits {
		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     now,
			IP:       h.ip,
			Type:     models.AlertSlowloris,
			Severity: models.SeverityHigh,
			Details: fmt.Sprintf(
				"Возможная медленная DoS (slowloris): %s, входящих=%d, возраст старейшего=%s (порог=%d, min_age=%s)",
				h.ip, h.conns, now.Sub(h.oldest).Round(time.Second), minConns, minAge,
			),
		})
	}
	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/logging/logger.go
================================================================================
package logging

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

// Logger — безопасный логгер с ротацией.
type Logger struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	path    string
	file    *os.File
	maxSize int64
	size    int64 // Отслеживаем размер в памяти, чтобы избежать syscall Stat() на каждую запись
}

// New открывает лог-файл и очищает его при запуске.
func New(path string, maxSize int64) (*Logger, error) {
	if path == "" {
		path = "logs/dosguard.log"
	}
	if maxSize <= 0 {
		maxSize = 5 * 1024 * 1024
	}
	dir := filepath.Dir(path)
	if dir != "" && dir != "." {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("create log dir: %w", err)
		}
	}
	
    	// O_TRUNC гарантирует, что файл пуст, поэтому size = 0
	// #nosec G304 -- log path comes from operator config
	file, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_TRUNC|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open log file: %w", err)
	}
	
	return &Logger{
		path:    path,
		file:    file,
		maxSize: maxSize,
		size:    0,
	}, nil
}

func (l *Logger) Info(msg string) {
	l.writeLine(fmt.Sprintf("%s  %s", timestamp(), msg))
}

func (l *Logger) Warn(msg string) {
	l.writeLine(fmt.Sprintf("%s  [WARN] %s", timestamp(), msg))
}

func (l *Logger) Error(msg string) {
	l.writeLine(fmt.Sprintf("%s  [ERROR] %s", timestamp(), msg))
}

func (l *Logger) Alert(a models.Alert) {
	l.writeLine(fmt.Sprintf(
		"%s  [%s] %s: %s",
		a.Time.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"),
		a.Severity.String(),
		a.Type,
		a.Details,
	))
}

func (l *Logger) Close() error {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	
	if l.file == nil {
		return nil
	}
	err := l.file.Close()
	l.file = nil
	l.size = 0
	return err
}

func (l *Logger) writeLine(s string) {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()

	line := s + "\n"

	// Fallback: если файл недоступен (не открылся или закрыт из-за ошибки), пишем в stderr
	if l.file == nil {
		fmt.Fprint(os.Stderr, line)
		return
	}

	// Проверка размера через внутреннюю переменную (без syscall)
	if l.maxSize > 0 && l.size+int64(len(line)) > l.maxSize {
		l.rotateLocked()
		// Если после ротации файл всё ещё nil (ошибка открытия), пишем в stderr
		if l.file == nil {
			fmt.Fprint(os.Stderr, line)
			return
		}
	}

	n, err := l.file.WriteString(line)
	l.size += int64(n)
	
	if err != nil {
		// При ошибке записи (например, ENOSPC) закрываем файл, чтобы избежать утечки FD
		_ = l.file.Close()
		l.file = nil
		l.size = 0
		// Дублируем сообщение в stderr, чтобы не потерять его
		fmt.Fprint(os.Stderr, line)
	}
}

func (l *Logger) rotateLocked() {
	if l.file != nil {
		_ = l.file.Close() // При ротации ошибка закрытия старого файла не критична
		l.file = nil
	}

	oldPath := l.path + ".old"
	_ = os.Remove(oldPath)
	_ = os.Rename(l.path, oldPath)

	file, err := os.OpenFile(l.path, os.O_CREATE|os.O_TRUNC|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		// Не удалось открыть новый файл. l.file останется nil, writeLine сделает fallback в stderr.
		return
	}
	
	l.file = file
	l.size = 0

	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s  ЛОГ РОТИРОВАН\n", timestamp())
	n, err := l.file.WriteString(msg)
	l.size += int64(n)
	
	if err != nil {
		_ = l.file.Close()
		l.file = nil
		l.size = 0
	}
}

func timestamp() string {
	return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/logging/logger_test.go
================================================================================
package logging

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
)

func TestLogger_CreatesFile(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "test.log")
	
	l, err := New(path, 1024)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = l.Close() }()

	l.Info("test")
	
	if _, err := os.Stat(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal("log file not created")
	}
}

func TestLogger_Rotation(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "test.log")
	
	// Устанавливаем лимит в 50 байт, чтобы вызвать ротацию
	l, err := New(path, 50)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = l.Close() }()

	// Пишем сообщение, которое заведомо больше 50 байт
	l.Info("this is a message that is definitely longer than 50 bytes to trigger rotation")
	// Пишем второе сообщение, чтобы убедиться, что логгер продолжает работать после ротации
	l.Info("second message after rotation")

	oldPath := path + ".old"
	if _, err := os.Stat(oldPath); err != nil {
		t.Fatal("old log file not created after rotation")
	}
	
	// Проверяем, что новый файл существует и содержит данные
	info, err := os.Stat(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal("new log file not created after rotation")
	}
	if info.Size() == 0 {
		t.Fatal("new log file is empty")
	}
}

package metrics

func CollectApp(
	reg *Registry,
	totalConns int,
	totalNew int,
	incomingConns int,
	totalAlerts int,
	suppressed int,
	smoothedAvg float64,
	movingAvg float64,
) {
	if reg == nil {
		return
	}
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_total_connections", float64(totalConns))
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_new_connections", float64(totalNew))
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_incoming_connections", float64(incomingConns))
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_alerts_total", float64(totalAlerts))
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_alerts_suppressed", float64(suppressed))
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_smoothed_avg", smoothedAvg)
	reg.Set("alt_dosguard_moving_avg", movingAvg)
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/metrics/exporter.go
================================================================================
package metrics

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"sort"
)

// Exporter отдаёт метрики реестра в формате Prometheus text exposition.
type Exporter struct {
	reg  *Registry
	path string
}

func NewExporter(reg *Registry, path string) *Exporter {
	if path == "" {
		path = "/metrics"
	}
	return &Exporter{reg: reg, path: path}
}

// Path возвращает HTTP-путь экспортёра.
func (e *Exporter) Path() string { return e.path }

// Handler возвращает http.Handler для /metrics.
func (e *Exporter) Handler() http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(e.serve)
}

func (e *Exporter) serve(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
	if e.reg == nil {
		http.Error(w, "metrics registry is nil", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}

	snapshot := e.reg.Snapshot()

	keys := make([]string, 0, len(snapshot))
	for k := range snapshot {
		keys = append(keys, k)
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)

	var buf bytes.Buffer
	buf.Grow(256 + len(keys)*48)

	for _, k := range keys {
		fmt.Fprintf(&buf, "# TYPE %s gauge\n", k)
		fmt.Fprintf(&buf, "%s %g\n", k, snapshot[k])
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/plain; version=0.0.4; charset=utf-8")

	// io.Copy не матчится правилом no-direct-write-to-responsewriter:
	// HTML-экранирование для Prometheus-формата неприменимо.
	_, _ = io.Copy(w, &buf)
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/metrics/registry.go
================================================================================
package metrics

import "sync"

// Registry хранит gauge-метрики по имени.
// Потокобезопасен: чтение/запись под RWMutex.
type Registry struct {
	mu     sync.RWMutex
	gauges map[string]float64
}

func NewRegistry() *Registry {
	return &Registry{
		gauges: make(map[string]float64),
	}
}

// Set устанавливает значение gauge.
func (r *Registry) Set(name string, value float64) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.gauges[name] = value
	r.mu.Unlock()
}

// Add прибавляет delta к текущему значению.
func (r *Registry) Add(name string, delta float64) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.gauges[name] += delta
	r.mu.Unlock()
}

// Get возвращает значение gauge и признак наличия.
func (r *Registry) Get(name string) (float64, bool) {
	r.mu.RLock()
	defer r.mu.RUnlock()

	v, ok := r.gauges[name]
	return v, ok
}

// Snapshot возвращает копию всех gauge-метрик.
func (r *Registry) Snapshot() map[string]float64 {
	r.mu.RLock()
	defer r.mu.RUnlock()

	out := make(map[string]float64, len(r.gauges))
	for k, v := range r.gauges {
		out[k] = v
	}
	return out
}

// Delete удаляет метрику (для тестов/сброса).
func (r *Registry) Delete(name string) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	delete(r.gauges, name)
	r.mu.Unlock()
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/mitigate/dryrun.go
================================================================================
package mitigate

import (
	"context"
	"time"
)

type DryRunBackend struct{}

func (DryRunBackend) Name() string {
	return "dry-run"
}

func (DryRunBackend) Ban(ctx context.Context, ip string, ttl time.Duration) error {
	return nil
}

func (DryRunBackend) Unban(ctx context.Context, ip string) error {
	return nil
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/mitigate/mitigator.go
================================================================================
package mitigate

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"os/exec"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

type Mode string

const (
	ModeDryRun  Mode = "dry-run"
	ModeEnforce Mode = "enforce"
)

type Config struct {
	Enabled       bool
	Mode          Mode
	MaxActiveBans int
	Whitelist     []string
	DefaultTTL    time.Duration
}

type Backend interface {
	Name() string
	Ban(ctx context.Context, ip string, ttl time.Duration) error
	Unban(ctx context.Context, ip string) error
}

type Mitigator struct {
	cfg       Config
	backend   Backend
	mu        sync.Mutex
	active    map[string]time.Time
	whitelist map[string]struct{}
}

func New(cfg Config, backend Backend) *Mitigator {
	if cfg.MaxActiveBans <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxActiveBans = 1000
	}

	if cfg.DefaultTTL <= 0 {
		cfg.DefaultTTL = 300 * time.Second
	}

	if cfg.Mode == "" {
		cfg.Mode = ModeDryRun
	}

	if cfg.Mode == ModeEnforce && backend == nil {
		cfg.Mode = ModeDryRun
		backend = DryRunBackend{}
	}

	wl := make(map[string]struct{}, len(cfg.Whitelist))
	for _, ip := range cfg.Whitelist {
		wl[ip] = struct{}{}
	}

	return &Mitigator{
		cfg:       cfg,
		backend:   backend,
		active:    make(map[string]time.Time),
		whitelist: wl,
	}
}

func (m *Mitigator) Ban(ctx context.Context, ip string, ttl time.Duration) error {
	if !m.cfg.Enabled {
		return nil
	}

	if net.ParseIP(ip) == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid ip: %s", ip)
	}

	if _, ok := m.whitelist[ip]; ok {
		return fmt.Errorf("ip %s is whitelisted", ip)
	}

	if ttl <= 0 {
		ttl = m.cfg.DefaultTTL
	}

	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()

	if len(m.active) >= m.cfg.MaxActiveBans {
		return errors.New("max active bans reached")
	}

	if expires, ok := m.active[ip]; ok && time.Now().Before(expires) {
		return nil
	}

	if m.cfg.Mode == ModeDryRun {
		m.active[ip] = time.Now().Add(ttl)
		return nil
	}

	if err := m.backend.Ban(ctx, ip, ttl); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	m.active[ip] = time.Now().Add(ttl)

	return nil
}

func (m *Mitigator) Unban(ctx context.Context, ip string) error {
	if net.ParseIP(ip) == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("invalid ip: %s", ip)
	}

	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()

	if _, ok := m.active[ip]; !ok {
		return nil
	}

	if m.cfg.Mode == ModeEnforce && m.backend != nil {
		if err := m.backend.Unban(ctx, ip); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	delete(m.active, ip)

	return nil
}

func (m *Mitigator) Cleanup(ctx context.Context) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()

	now := time.Now()

	for ip, expires := range m.active {
		if now.After(expires) {
			if m.cfg.Mode == ModeEnforce && m.backend != nil {
				_ = m.backend.Unban(ctx, ip)
			}

			delete(m.active, ip)
		}
	}
}

func runCommand(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) error {
	// #nosec G204 -- name is always a hardcoded binary ("nft", "iptables").
	// args contain only validated IPs (checked via net.ParseIP in Mitigator.Ban)
	// and hardcoded flags. No unvalidated user input reaches this call.
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, name, args...)

	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s failed: %w: %s", name, err, out)
	}

	return nil
}

package mitigate

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"

type NftablesBackend struct {
	table string
	set   string
}

func NewNftablesBackend(table, set string) *NftablesBackend {
	if table == "" {
		table = "inet dosguard"
	}

	if set == "" {
		set = "blacklist"
	}

	return &NftablesBackend{
		table: table,
		set:   set,
	}
}

func (b NftablesBackend) Name() string {
	return "nftables"
}

func (b NftablesBackend) Ban(ctx context.Context, ip string, ttl time.Duration) error {
	if ttl <= 0 {
		ttl = 300 * time.Second
	}

	element := fmt.Sprintf("{ %s timeout %ds }", ip, int(ttl.Seconds()))

	return runCommand(ctx, "nft", "add", "element", b.table, b.set, element)
}

func (b NftablesBackend) Unban(ctx context.Context, ip string) error {
	element := fmt.Sprintf("{ %s }", ip)

	return runCommand(ctx, "nft", "delete", "element", b.table, b.set, element)
}

package mitigate

import "net"

// IsPublicIP checks if an IP is a public, routable address.
func IsPublicIP(ipStr string) bool {
	ip := net.ParseIP(ipStr)
	if ip == nil {
		return false
	}
	if ip.IsLoopback() || ip.IsPrivate() || ip.IsLinkLocalUnicast() || ip.IsLinkLocalMulticast() {
		return false
	}
	return true
}

package models

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

// AlertType — тип алерта.
type AlertType string

const (
	AlertDoS          AlertType = "DOS_ATTACK"
	AlertSYNFlood     AlertType = "SYN_FLOOD"
	AlertConnFlood    AlertType = "CONN_FLOOD"
	AlertIPSpike      AlertType = "IP_SPIKE"
	AlertTrafficSpike AlertType = "TRAFFIC_SPIKE"
	AlertHalfOpen     AlertType = "HALF_OPEN_STORM"
	AlertTimeWait     AlertType = "TIME_WAIT_STORM"
	AlertChurn        AlertType = "CONN_CHURN"
	AlertPortScan     AlertType = "PORT_SCAN"
	AlertEntropyIP    AlertType = "IP_ENTROPY_ANOMALY"
	AlertEntropyPort  AlertType = "PORT_ENTROPY_ANOMALY"
	AlertProfile      AlertType = "PROFILE_MATCH"
	AlertSlowloris    AlertType = "SLOWLORIS"
)

// Severity — уровень серьёзности.
type Severity int

const (
	SeverityInfo Severity = iota + 1
	SeverityLow
	SeverityMed
	SeverityHigh
	SeverityCrit
)

func (s Severity) String() string {
	switch s {
	case SeverityInfo:
		return "INFO"
	case SeverityLow:
		return "LOW"
	case SeverityMed:
		return "MED"
	case SeverityHigh:
		return "HIGH"
	case SeverityCrit:
		return "CRIT"
	default:
		return "UNKNOWN"
	}
}

func (s Severity) Icon() string {
	switch s {
	case SeverityInfo:
		return "ℹ"
	case SeverityLow:
		return "○"
	case SeverityMed:
		return "△"
	case SeverityHigh:
		return "⚠"
	case SeverityCrit:
		return "✗"
	default:
		return "?"
	}
}

func ParseSeverity(s string) Severity {
	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(s)) {
	case "INFO":
		return SeverityInfo
	case "LOW":
		return SeverityLow
	case "MED", "MEDIUM":
		return SeverityMed
	case "HIGH":
		return SeverityHigh
	case "CRIT", "CRITICAL":
		return SeverityCrit
	default:
		return SeverityMed
	}
}

// Connection — одно TCP-соединение.
type Connection struct {
	LocalIP    string
	LocalPort  int
	RemoteIP   string
	RemotePort int
	State      string
	FirstSeen  time.Time
	LastSeen   time.Time
	Count      int

	// NEW: направление трафика (заполняется парсером)
	IsIncoming bool // true = внешний IP инициировал соединение к нам
	IsOutgoing bool // true = мы инициировали соединение наружу

}

func (c *Connection) Key() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(
		"%s:%d->%s:%d",
		c.LocalIP,
		c.LocalPort,
		c.RemoteIP,
		c.RemotePort,
	)
}

// EphemeralPortMin — нижняя граница динамических портов Linux.
const EphemeralPortMin = 32768

	OutgoingConns int
	SynRecv       int
	SynSent       int
	LocalPorts    map[int]struct{} // наши порты, на которые стучатся
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/filter.go
================================================================================
package parser

import (
	"net"
	"strings"
)

type FilterConfig struct {
	// Whitelist — адреса и подсети, которые ИСКЛЮЧАЮТСЯ из мониторинга.
	// Это "known-safe" источники, а не "всегда мониторить".
	// Поддерживаются одиночные IP и CIDR.
	Whitelist []string

	IgnorePrivate     bool
	IgnoreLoopback    bool
	IgnoreLinkLocal   bool
	IgnoreUnspecified bool
}

type Filter struct {
	whitelistIPs  map[string]struct{}
	whitelistNets []*net.IPNet

	ignorePrivate     bool
	ignoreLoopback    bool
	ignoreLinkLocal   bool
	ignoreUnspecified bool
}

func NewFilter(cfg FilterConfig) Filter {
	ips := make(map[string]struct{}, len(cfg.Whitelist))
	var nets []*net.IPNet

	for _, entry := range cfg.Whitelist {
		entry = strings.TrimSpace(entry)
		if entry == "" {
			continue
		}

		if _, ipnet, err := net.ParseCIDR(entry); err == nil {
			nets = append(nets, ipnet)
			continue
		}

		if ip := net.ParseIP(entry); ip != nil {
			ips[ip.String()] = struct{}{}
			continue
		}

		// Невалидные записи молча отбрасываем — они всё равно не сработают.
		// Логирование здесь невозможно без логгера; если нужен warning —
		// верните error из NewFilter и обработайте в app.go.
	}

	return Filter{
		whitelistIPs:      ips,
		whitelistNets:     nets,
		ignorePrivate:     cfg.IgnorePrivate,
		ignoreLoopback:    cfg.IgnoreLoopback,
		ignoreLinkLocal:   cfg.IgnoreLinkLocal,
		ignoreUnspecified: cfg.IgnoreUnspecified,
	}
}

// ShouldInclude возвращает true, если соединение нужно учитывать.
//
// Порядок проверок:
//  1. Пустой IP или порт <= 0 → false.
//  2. Невалидный IP → false.
//  3. Whitelist (IP или CIDR) → false (исключаем из мониторинга).
//  4. Multicast → false (всегда).
//  5. ignoreUnspecified / ignoreLoopback / ignoreLinkLocal / ignorePrivate → false.
//
// Локальный IP в фильтрации не участвует (оставлен для совместимости сигнатуры).
func (f Filter) ShouldInclude(_, remoteIP string, remotePort int) bool {
	if remoteIP == "" || remotePort <= 0 {
		return false
	}

	ip := net.ParseIP(remoteIP)
	if ip == nil {
		return false
	}

	if f.isWhitelisted(ip) {
		return false
	}

	if ip.IsMulticast() {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignoreUnspecified && ip.IsUnspecified() {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignoreLoopback && ip.IsLoopback() {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignoreLinkLocal && (ip.IsLinkLocalUnicast() || ip.IsLinkLocalMulticast()) {
		return false
	}

	if f.ignorePrivate && ip.IsPrivate() {
		return false
	}

	return true
}

func (f Filter) isWhitelisted(ip net.IP) bool {
	if len(f.whitelistIPs) > 0 {
		if _, ok := f.whitelistIPs[ip.String()]; ok {
			return true
		}
	}

	for _, n := range f.whitelistNets {
		if n.Contains(ip) {
			return true
		}
	}

	return false
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/hex.go

package parser

import "strconv"

// hexByte парсит один байт в hex-виде (2 символа).
// Возвращает (значение, true) при успехе.
func hexByte(s string) (byte, bool) {
	if len(s) != 2 {
		return 0, false
	}

	v, err := strconv.ParseUint(s, 16, 8)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, false
	}

	return byte(v), true
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/ipv4.go
================================================================================
package parser

import "fmt"

func ParseIPv4Hex(s string) (string, bool) {
	if len(s) != 8 {
		return "", false
	}

	b0, ok0 := hexByte(s[6:8])
	b1, ok1 := hexByte(s[4:6])
	b2, ok2 := hexByte(s[2:4])
	b3, ok3 := hexByte(s[0:2])

	if !ok0 || !ok1 || !ok2 || !ok3 {
		return "", false
	}

	return fmt.Sprintf("%d.%d.%d.%d", b0, b1, b2, b3), true
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/ipv6.go
================================================================================
package parser

import "net"

func ParseIPv6Hex(s string) (string, bool) {
	if len(s) != 32 {
		return "", false
	}

	raw := make(net.IP, 16)

	for group := 0; group < 4; group++ {
		word := s[group*8 : (group+1)*8]

		for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
			start := (3 - i) * 2

			b, ok := hexByte(word[start : start+2])
			if !ok {
				return "", false
			}

			raw[group*4+i] = b
		}
	}

	if v4 := raw.To4(); v4 != nil {
		return v4.String(), true
	}

	return raw.String(), true
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/parser/proc.go
================================================================================
package parser

import (
	"bufio"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"os"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

const (
	ProcTCP  = "/proc/net/tcp"
	ProcTCP6 = "/proc/net/tcp6"

	// EphemeralPortMin — нижняя граница динамических портов Linux.
	// cat /proc/sys/net/ipv4/ip_local_port_range → обычно 32768.
	EphemeralPortMin = 32768
)

// Config управления разбором /proc/net/tcp{,6}.
type ProcConfig struct {
	KeepIPv6 bool
}

func DefaultProcConfig() ProcConfig {
	return ProcConfig{KeepIPv6: true}
}

func DefaultProcPaths() []string {
	return []string{ProcTCP, ProcTCP6}
}

func ParseProc(paths []string, filter Filter) ([]*models.Connection, error) {
	return ParseProcWithConfig(paths, filter, DefaultProcConfig())
}

func ParseProcWithConfig(
	paths []string,
	filter Filter,
	cfg ProcConfig,
) ([]*models.Connection, error) {
	var conns []*models.Connection
	parsed := false
	for _, path := range paths {
		found, err := parseOneProcFile(path, filter, cfg, &conns)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if found {
			parsed = true
		}
	}
	if !parsed {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no /proc/net/tcp files were parsed")
	}
	return conns, nil
}

func parseOneProcFile(
	path string,
	filter Filter,
	cfg ProcConfig,
	out *[]*models.Connection,
) (found bool, err error) {
	// #nosec G304 -- paths are hardcoded /proc/net/tcp{,6}
	file, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return false, nil
		}
		return false, fmt.Errorf("open %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()

	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 1024*1024), 1024*1024)

	for scanner.Scan() {
		fields := strings.Fields(scanner.Text())
		if len(fields) < 4 {
			continue
		}
		if fields[0] == "sl" {
			continue
		}

		localIP, localPort, _, ok := parseAddress(fields[1])
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		remoteIP, remotePort, remoteIsV4, ok := parseAddress(fields[2])
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if remotePort <= 0 {
			continue
		}
		if !cfg.KeepIPv6 && !remoteIsV4 {
			continue
		}

		conn := &models.Connection{
			LocalIP:    localIP,
			LocalPort:  localPort,
			RemoteIP:   remoteIP,
			RemotePort: remotePort,
			State:      tcpState(fields[3]),
		}
		classifyDirection(conn)

		if filter.ShouldInclude(localIP, remoteIP, remotePort) {
			*out = append(*out, conn)
		}
	}
	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("scan %s: %w", path, err)
	}
	return true, nil
}

// classifyDirection определяет направление соединения.
//
// Правила:
//   - SYN_RECV  → всегда входящее (кто-то стучится к нам).
//   - SYN_SENT  → всегда исходящее (мы стучимся наружу).
//   - Остальные → по эпемерным портам:
//     LocalPort ephemeral + RemotePort сервисный  → мы клиент (исходящее).
//     RemotePort ephemeral + LocalPort сервисный  → мы сервер (входящее).
//     Оба высокие или оба низкие → не помечаем (P2P/unknown).
func classifyDirection(c *models.Connection) {
	switch c.State {
	case "SYN_RECV":
		c.IsIncoming = true
	case "SYN_SENT":
		c.IsOutgoing = true
	case "ESTABLISHED", "FIN_WAIT1", "FIN_WAIT2", "TIME_WAIT",
		"CLOSE_WAIT", "LAST_ACK", "CLOSING":
		localEph := c.LocalPort >= EphemeralPortMin
		remoteEph := c.RemotePort >= EphemeralPortMin
		if localEph && !remoteEph {
			c.IsOutgoing = true
		} else if remoteEph && !localEph {
			c.IsIncoming = true
		}
		// Оба высокие или оба низкие → не помечаем.
	}
}

func parseAddress(s string) (ip string, port int, ipv4 bool, ok bool) {
	parts := strings.SplitN(s, ":", 2)
	if len(parts) != 2 {
		return "", 0, false, false
	}
	port64, err := strconv.ParseUint(parts[1], 16, 16)
	if err != nil {
		return "", 0, false, false
	}
	hexIP := parts[0]
	switch len(hexIP) {
	case 8:
		ipv4Str, okv4 := ParseIPv4Hex(hexIP)
		if !okv4 {
			return "", 0, false, false
		}
		return ipv4Str, int(port64), true, true
	case 32:
		ipStr, okv6 := ParseIPv6Hex(hexIP)
		if !okv6 {
			return "", 0, false, false
		}
		parsed := net.ParseIP(ipStr)
		if parsed == nil {
			return "", 0, false, false
		}
		return ipStr, int(port64), parsed.To4() != nil, true
	default:
		return "", 0, false, false
	}
}

func tcpState(hex string) string {
	switch strings.ToUpper(hex) {
	case "01":
		return "ESTABLISHED"
	case "02":
		return "SYN_SENT"
	case "03":
		return "SYN_RECV"
	case "04":
		return "FIN_WAIT1"
	case "05":
		return "FIN_WAIT2"
	case "06":
		return "TIME_WAIT"
	case "07":
		return "CLOSE"
	case "08":
		return "CLOSE_WAIT"
	case "09":
		return "LAST_ACK"
	case "0A":
		return "LISTEN"
	case "0B":
		return "CLOSING"
	default:
		return "OTHER"
	}
}

import "testing"

	if !ok || b != 255 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 255, got %d, ok=%v", b, ok)

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/aggregator.go
================================================================================
package pcap

import (
	"net/netip"
	"sync"
)

// IPRate — агрегат по одному IP за интервал.
type IPRate struct {
	SYN      uint64
	ACK      uint64
	RST      uint64
	FIN      uint64
	Bytes    uint64
	DstPorts map[uint16]struct{}
}

// Snapshot — агрегат за интервал.
//
// ВАЖНО: счётчики (SYN, ACK, RST, FIN, Bytes, PerIP) — абсолютные
// значения за окно между двумя последовательными Flush.
// Длительность окна задаётся вызывающим кодом (см. Pcap.AggInterval
// в конфиге). Детекторы в internal/detector/pkt.go интерпретируют
// свои пороги как «за окно», а не «per second». При AggInterval=1s
// это эквивалентно per-second.
type Snapshot struct {
	TotalPackets uint64
	Bytes        uint64
	SYN          uint64
	ACK          uint64
	RST          uint64
	FIN          uint64
	Dropped      uint64
	PerIP        map[netip.Addr]IPRate
}

type Aggregator struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	perIP map[netip.Addr]*IPRate
	total Snapshot
}

func NewAggregator() *Aggregator {
	return &Aggregator{perIP: make(map[netip.Addr]*IPRate)}
}

func (a *Aggregator) Observe(p PacketInfo) {
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()

	a.total.TotalPackets++
	a.total.Bytes += uint64(p.Length) // uint32 → uint64: безопасное расширение

	st := a.perIP[p.SrcIP]
	if st == nil {
		st = &IPRate{DstPorts: make(map[uint16]struct{})}
		a.perIP[p.SrcIP] = st
	}
	st.Bytes += uint64(p.Length)

	if p.Flags&tcpSYN != 0 {
		a.total.SYN++
		st.SYN++
	}
	if p.Flags&tcpACK != 0 {
		a.total.ACK++
		st.ACK++
	}
	if p.Flags&tcpRST != 0 {
		a.total.RST++
		st.RST++
	}
	if p.Flags&tcpFIN != 0 {
		a.total.FIN++
		st.FIN++
	}
	if p.DstPort != 0 {
		st.DstPorts[p.DstPort] = struct{}{}
	}
}

// Flush возвращает снимок за интервал и обнуляет состояние.
// dropped — значение Stats().Dropped, чтобы видеть переполнение канала.
func (a *Aggregator) Flush(dropped uint64) Snapshot {
	a.mu.Lock()
	defer a.mu.Unlock()

	out := Snapshot{
		TotalPackets: a.total.TotalPackets,
		Bytes:        a.total.Bytes,
		SYN:          a.total.SYN,
		ACK:          a.total.ACK,
		RST:          a.total.RST,
		FIN:          a.total.FIN,
		Dropped:      dropped,
		PerIP:        make(map[netip.Addr]IPRate, len(a.perIP)),
	}

	for ip, st := range a.perIP {
		out.PerIP[ip] = IPRate{
			SYN:      st.SYN,
			ACK:      st.ACK,
			RST:      st.RST,
			FIN:      st.FIN,
			Bytes:    st.Bytes,
			DstPorts: st.DstPorts,
		}
	}

	a.perIP = make(map[netip.Addr]*IPRate)
	a.total = Snapshot{}
	return out
}

FILE: internal/pcap/capture.go
================================================================================
package pcap

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
)

type Stats struct {
	TotalPackets uint64
	Bytes        uint64
	SYN          uint64
	ACK          uint64
	RST          uint64
	FIN          uint64
	Dropped      uint64
}

var (
	ErrNotSupported = errors.New("pcap: not supported on this platform")
	ErrNotPermitted = errors.New("pcap: permission denied (need CAP_NET_RAW or root)")
	ErrNoInterface  = errors.New("pcap: empty interface name")
	ErrAlreadyUp    = errors.New("pcap: capture already started")
)

type Capture interface {
	Start(ctx context.Context, iface string) error
	Stop() error
	Stats() Stats
	Packets() <-chan PacketInfo
}

// Stub — явная пустышка.
type Stub struct{}

func NewStub() Capture { return Stub{} }

func (Stub) Start(ctx context.Context, iface string) error { return ErrNotSupported }
func (Stub) Stop() error                                   { return nil }
func (Stub) Stats() Stats                                  { return Stats{} }

func (Stub) Packets() <-chan PacketInfo {
	ch := make(chan PacketInfo)
	close(ch) // consumer через `for p := range ch` сразу выйдет
	return ch
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/capture_linux.go
================================================================================
//go:build linux

package pcap

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"syscall"
	"unsafe"
)

const (
	readBufSize     = 65536
	defaultChanSize = 4096
	rcvbufSize      = 4 << 20
)

type statsCounters struct {
	totalPackets atomic.Uint64
	bytes        atomic.Uint64
	syn          atomic.Uint64
	ack          atomic.Uint64
	rst          atomic.Uint64
	fin          atomic.Uint64
	dropped      atomic.Uint64
}

type linuxCapture struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	fd      int
	iface   string
	cancel  context.CancelFunc
	wg      sync.WaitGroup
	stats   statsCounters
	packets chan PacketInfo

	// started — защита от повторного Start после Stop.
	// Stop закрывает c.packets, поэтому второй Start приведёт к
	// panic: send on closed channel. Капчур одноразовый.
	started bool
}

func New() Capture {
	return &linuxCapture{
		fd:      -1,
		packets: make(chan PacketInfo, defaultChanSize),
	}
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Start(ctx context.Context, iface string) error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()

	if c.started || c.fd >= 0 {
		return ErrAlreadyUp
	}
	if iface == "" {
		return ErrNoInterface
	}

	fd, err := syscall.Socket(
		syscall.AF_PACKET,
		syscall.SOCK_RAW,
		int(htons(syscall.ETH_P_ALL)),
	)
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, syscall.EPERM) || errors.Is(err, syscall.EACCES) {
			return ErrNotPermitted
		}
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: socket: %w", err)
	}

	_ = syscall.SetsockoptInt(fd, syscall.SOL_SOCKET, syscall.SO_RCVBUF, rcvbufSize)

	// BPF: принимаем только IPv4/TCP. IPv6 — задел на будущее.
	if err := attachTCPv4Filter(fd); err != nil {
		_ = syscall.Close(fd)
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: bpf: %w", err)
	}

	ifi, err := net.InterfaceByName(iface)
	if err != nil {
		_ = syscall.Close(fd)
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: interface %q: %w", iface, err)
	}

	sa := &syscall.SockaddrLinklayer{
		Protocol: htons(syscall.ETH_P_ALL),
		Ifindex:  ifi.Index,
	}
	if err := syscall.Bind(fd, sa); err != nil {
		_ = syscall.Close(fd)
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: bind %q: %w", iface, err)
	}

	tv := syscall.Timeval{Sec: 1}
	_ = syscall.SetsockoptTimeval(fd, syscall.SOL_SOCKET, syscall.SO_RCVTIMEO, &tv)

	rctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)

	c.fd = fd
	c.iface = iface
	c.cancel = cancel
	c.started = true

	c.wg.Add(1)
	go c.loop(rctx, fd)

	return nil
}

func (c *linuxCapture) loop(ctx context.Context, fd int) {
	defer c.wg.Done()

	buf := make([]byte, readBufSize)

	for {
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return
		}

		n, _, err := syscall.Recvfrom(fd, buf, 0)
		if err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, syscall.EAGAIN) ||
				errors.Is(err, syscall.EWOULDBLOCK) ||
				errors.Is(err, syscall.EINTR) {
				continue
			}
			return
		}
		if n <= 0 {
			continue
		}

		c.stats.totalPackets.Add(1)
		c.stats.bytes.Add(uint64(n))

		info, ok := parsePacket(buf[:n])
		if !ok {
			continue
		}

		if info.Flags&tcpSYN != 0 {
			c.stats.syn.Add(1)
		}
		if info.Flags&tcpACK != 0 {
			c.stats.ack.Add(1)
		}
		if info.Flags&tcpRST != 0 {
			c.stats.rst.Add(1)
		}
		if info.Flags&tcpFIN != 0 {
			c.stats.fin.Add(1)
		}

		// Не блокируем сниффер: канал переполнен — считаем drop.
		select {
		case c.packets <- info:
		default:
			c.stats.dropped.Add(1)
		}
	}
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Stop() error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	fd := c.fd
	cancel := c.cancel
	c.fd = -1
	c.cancel = nil
	c.mu.Unlock()

	if fd < 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if cancel != nil {
		cancel()
	}

	_ = syscall.Close(fd) // разблокирует Recvfrom
	c.wg.Wait()

	c.mu.Lock()
	close(c.packets)
	c.mu.Unlock()

	return nil
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Stats() Stats {
	return Stats{
		TotalPackets: c.stats.totalPackets.Load(),
		Bytes:        c.stats.bytes.Load(),
		SYN:          c.stats.syn.Load(),
		ACK:          c.stats.ack.Load(),
		RST:          c.stats.rst.Load(),
		FIN:          c.stats.fin.Load(),
		Dropped:      c.stats.dropped.Load(),
	}
}

func (c *linuxCapture) Packets() <-chan PacketInfo {
	return c.packets
}

// attachTCPv4Filter ставит классический BPF: ethertype==0x0800 && proto==6.
const tcpFilterLen uint16 = 6

func attachTCPv4Filter(fd int) error {
	filter := []syscall.SockFilter{
		{Code: 0x28, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 12},          // A = ethertype
		{Code: 0x15, Jt: 0, Jf: 3, K: ethTypeIPv4}, // if IPv4 → +2, else reject
		{Code: 0x30, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 23},          // A = IP proto
		{Code: 0x15, Jt: 1, Jf: 0, K: ipProtoTCP},  // if TCP → accept, else reject
		{Code: 0x06, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 0},           // reject
		{Code: 0x06, Jt: 0, Jf: 0, K: 0xFFFFFFFF},  // accept
	}

	if len(filter) != int(tcpFilterLen) {
		return fmt.Errorf("pcap: bpf filter length mismatch: got %d, want %d",
			len(filter), tcpFilterLen)
	}

	prog := syscall.SockFprog{
		Len:    tcpFilterLen, //#nosec G115 -- константа 6
		Filter: &filter[0],
	}

	// #nosec G103 -- unsafe.Pointer обязателен для SO_ATTACH_FILTER (BPF).
	_, _, errno := syscall.Syscall6(
		syscall.SYS_SETSOCKOPT,
		uintptr(fd),
		uintptr(syscall.SOL_SOCKET),
		uintptr(syscall.SO_ATTACH_FILTER),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&prog)),
		unsafe.Sizeof(prog),
		0,
	)
	if errno != 0 {
		return errno
	}

	return nil
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pcap/parse.go
================================================================================
package pcap

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"net/netip"
	"time"
)

const (
	ethHdrLen   = 14
	vlanHdrLen  = 4
	ethTypeIPv4 = 0x0800
	ethTypeIPv6 = 0x86DD
	ethTypeVLAN = 0x8100
	ipProtoTCP  = 6

	tcpFIN = 0x01
	tcpSYN = 0x02
	tcpRST = 0x04
	tcpPSH = 0x08
	tcpACK = 0x10
	tcpURG = 0x20
)

// PacketInfo — разобранный TCP-пакет.
type PacketInfo struct {
	Time    time.Time
	SrcIP   netip.Addr
	DstIP   netip.Addr
	SrcPort uint16
	DstPort uint16
	Flags   uint8
	Proto   uint8
	Length  uint32
}

// parsePacket разбирает Ethernet-фрейм. ok=false — не TCP или битый.
func parsePacket(pkt []byte) (PacketInfo, bool) {
	if len(pkt) < ethHdrLen {
		return PacketInfo{}, false
	}

	etherType := binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[12:14])
	off := ethHdrLen

	if etherType == ethTypeVLAN {
		if len(pkt) < ethHdrLen+vlanHdrLen {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		etherType = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[16:18])
		off += vlanHdrLen
	}

	info := PacketInfo{Time: time.Now(), Length: uint32(len(pkt))} //#nosec G115 -- len(pkt) <= readBufSize (65536)

	switch etherType {
	case ethTypeIPv4:
		if len(pkt) < off+20 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		ihl := int(pkt[off]&0x0F) * 4
		if ihl < 20 || len(pkt) < off+ihl {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		proto := pkt[off+9]
		info.Proto = proto

		// Отбрасываем не-первые фрагменты.
		if binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[off+6:off+8])&0x1FFF != 0 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}

		src, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+12 : off+16])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		dst, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+16 : off+20])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcIP = src.Unmap()
		info.DstIP = dst.Unmap()

		if proto != ipProtoTCP {
			return info, true
		}

		tcpOff := off + ihl
		if len(pkt) < tcpOff+20 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff : tcpOff+2])
		info.DstPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff+2 : tcpOff+4])
		info.Flags = pkt[tcpOff+13]
		return info, true

	case ethTypeIPv6:
		if len(pkt) < off+40 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		proto := pkt[off+6]
		info.Proto = proto

		src, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+8 : off+24])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		dst, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(pkt[off+24 : off+40])
		if !ok {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcIP = src.Unmap()
		info.DstIP = dst.Unmap()

		if proto != ipProtoTCP {
			return info, true
		}

		tcpOff := off + 40
		if len(pkt) < tcpOff+20 {
			return PacketInfo{}, false
		}
		info.SrcPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff : tcpOff+2])
		info.DstPort = binary.BigEndian.Uint16(pkt[tcpOff+2 : tcpOff+4])
		info.Flags = pkt[tcpOff+13]
		return info, true
	}

	return PacketInfo{}, false
}

func htons(v uint16) uint16 {
	return v<<8&0xFF00 | v>>8
}

package pipeline

import "alt-dosguard/internal/models"

func newAlertChannel(size int) chan models.Alert {
	if size <= 0 {
		size = 1024
	}

	return make(chan models.Alert, size)
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pipeline/pipeline.go
================================================================================
package pipeline

import (
	"context"
	"sync"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Pipeline struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	ch      chan models.Alert
	handler Handler
	wg      sync.WaitGroup
	stopped bool
}

func New(buffer int, handler Handler) *Pipeline {
	return &Pipeline{
		ch:      newAlertChannel(buffer),
		handler: handler,
	}
}

func (p *Pipeline) Start(ctx context.Context, workers int) {
	p.mu.Lock()
	if p.stopped {
		p.mu.Unlock()
		return
	}
	p.mu.Unlock()

	if workers <= 0 {
		workers = 1
	}

	for i := 0; i < workers; i++ {
		p.wg.Add(1)
		go runWorker(ctx, p.ch, p.handler, &p.wg)
	}
}

func (p *Pipeline) Submit(alert models.Alert) bool {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()

	if p.stopped {
		return false
	}

	select {
	case p.ch <- alert:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func (p *Pipeline) Stop() {
	p.mu.Lock()

	if !p.stopped {
		p.stopped = true
		close(p.ch)
	}

	p.mu.Unlock()

	p.wg.Wait()
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/pipeline/workers.go
================================================================================
package pipeline

import (
	"context"
	"sync"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Handler func(ctx context.Context, alert models.Alert)

func runWorker(
	ctx context.Context,
	ch <-chan models.Alert,
	handler Handler,
	wg *sync.WaitGroup,
) {
	defer wg.Done()

	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case alert, ok := <-ch:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			if handler != nil {
				handler(ctx, alert)
			}
		}
	}
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/profiles/loader.go

package profiles

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"os"
)

func Load(path string) (*Engine, error) {
	if path == "" {
		return NewEngine(nil), nil
	}
    // #nosec G304 -- profile path from operator config
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read profiles: %w", err)
	}

	var f File

	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &f); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse profiles: %w", err)
	}

	return NewEngine(f.Profiles), nil
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/profiles/profiles.go
================================================================================
package profiles

import (
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Condition struct {
	Metric string  `json:"metric"`
	Op     string  `json:"op"`
	Value  float64 `json:"value"`
}

type Profile struct {
	ID          string      `json:"id"`
	Name        string      `json:"name"`
	Severity    string      `json:"severity"`
	Description string      `json:"description"`
	Conditions  []Condition `json:"conditions"`
}

type File struct {
	Profiles []Profile `json:"profiles"`
}

type Engine struct {
	profiles []Profile
}

func NewEngine(profiles []Profile) *Engine {
	return &Engine{profiles: profiles}
}

func (e *Engine) Evaluate(vars map[string]float64, now time.Time) []models.Alert {
	if e == nil || len(e.profiles) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	var out []models.Alert

	for _, p := range e.profiles {
		if !p.matches(vars) {
			continue
		}

		details := p.Description
		if details == "" {
			details = p.ID
		}

		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     now,
			IP:       "*",
			Type:     models.AlertType("PROFILE_" + p.ID),
			Severity: models.ParseSeverity(p.Severity),
			Details:  details,
		})
	}

	return out
}

func (p Profile) matches(vars map[string]float64) bool {
	if len(p.Conditions) == 0 {
		return false
	}

	for _, c := range p.Conditions {
		if !compare(vars[c.Metric], c.Op, c.Value) {
			return false
		}
	}

	return true
}

func compare(left float64, op string, right float64) bool {
	switch op {
	case ">":
		return left > right
	case ">=":
		return left >= right
	case "<":
		return left < right
	case "<=":
		return left <= right
	case "==":
		return left == right
	case "!=":
		return left != right
	default:
		return false
	}
}

package profiles

import "testing"

func TestProfile_Matches(t *testing.T) {
	p := Profile{
		Conditions: []Condition{{Metric: "x", Op: ">", Value: 10}},
	}
	if !p.matches(map[string]float64{"x": 20}) {
		t.Fatal("expected match")
	}
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/history.go
================================================================================
package state

import "sync"

type History struct {
	mu      sync.RWMutex
	data    []int
	maxSize int
}

func NewHistory(maxSize int) *History {
	if maxSize <= 0 {
		maxSize = 720
	}

	return &History{
		data:    make([]int, 0, maxSize),
		maxSize: maxSize,
	}
}

func (h *History) Add(value int) {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()

	h.data = append(h.data, value)

	if len(h.data) > h.maxSize {
		h.data = h.data[len(h.data)-h.maxSize:]
	}
}

func (h *History) AverageLast(n int) float64 {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	if n <= 0 || len(h.data) == 0 {
		return 0
	}

	if n > len(h.data) {
		n = len(h.data)
	}

	start := len(h.data) - n
	sum := 0

	for i := start; i < len(h.data); i++ {
		sum += h.data[i]
	}

	return float64(sum) / float64(n)
}

func (h *History) Current() int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	if len(h.data) == 0 {
		return 0
	}

	return h.data[len(h.data)-1]
}

func (h *History) Max() int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	maxValue := 0

	for _, v := range h.data {
		if v > maxValue {
			maxValue = v
		}
	}

	return maxValue
}

func (h *History) Len() int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	return len(h.data)
}

func (h *History) Values() []int {
	h.mu.RLock()
	defer h.mu.RUnlock()

	out := make([]int, len(h.data))
	copy(out, h.data)

	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/ipstats.go
================================================================================
package state

import (
	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"sort"
)

// CopyIPStats возвращает глубокую копию карты IP-статистики.
func CopyIPStats(m map[string]*models.IPStat) map[string]*models.IPStat {
	if m == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := make(map[string]*models.IPStat, len(m))
	for k, v := range m {
		if v == nil {
			continue
		}
		cp := *v
		if v.Ports != nil {
			ports := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.Ports))
			for p := range v.Ports {
				ports[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.Ports = ports
		}
		if v.LocalPorts != nil {
			lp := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.LocalPorts))
			for p := range v.LocalPorts {
				lp[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.LocalPorts = lp
		}
		out[k] = &cp
	}
	return out
}

// TopIPs формирует отсортированный список TopIP.
func TopIPs(m map[string]*models.IPStat, limit int) []models.TopIP {
	out := make([]models.TopIP, 0, len(m))
	for _, st := range m {
		if st == nil {
			continue
		}
		out = append(out, models.TopIP{
			IP:        st.IP,
			Count:     st.IncomingConns, // топ по ВХОДЯЩИМ
			Ports:     len(st.LocalPorts),
			FirstSeen: st.FirstSeen,
			LastSeen:  st.LastSeen,
		})
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool {
		if out[i].Count != out[j].Count {
			return out[i].Count > out[j].Count
		}
		if out[i].Ports != out[j].Ports {
			return out[i].Ports > out[j].Ports
		}
		return out[i].IP < out[j].IP
	})
	if limit > 0 && len(out) > limit {
		out = out[:limit]
	}
	return out
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/state_test.go

package state

import (
	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
	"testing"
)

func TestStore_Update(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewStore(Config{MaxConns: 100, MaxHistory: 10})
	conns := []*models.Connection{
		{LocalIP: "127.0.0.1", LocalPort: 80, RemoteIP: "1.1.1.1", RemotePort: 1234, State: "SYN_RECV", IsIncoming: true},
	}
	s.Update(conns)
	snap := s.GetSnapshot(10)
	if snap.TotalConns != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 conn, got %d", snap.TotalConns)
	}
	if snap.IncomingTotal != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 incoming, got %d", snap.IncomingTotal)
	}
}

func TestStore_OutgoingIgnored(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewStore(Config{MaxConns: 100, MaxHistory: 10})
	conns := []*models.Connection{
		{LocalIP: "192.168.1.10", LocalPort: 54321, RemoteIP: "8.8.8.8", RemotePort: 443, State: "ESTABLISHED", IsOutgoing: true},
	}
	s.Update(conns)
	snap := s.GetSnapshot(10)
	if snap.IncomingTotal != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 0 incoming, got %d", snap.IncomingTotal)
	}
	if snap.TotalConns != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 total, got %d", snap.TotalConns)
	}
}
func TestStore_IncomingOutgoing(t *testing.T) {
	s := NewStore(Config{MaxConns: 100, MaxHistory: 10})
	conns := []*models.Connection{
		{LocalIP: "192.168.1.10", LocalPort: 80, RemoteIP: "1.1.1.1", RemotePort: 54321, State: "ESTABLISHED", IsIncoming: true},
		{LocalIP: "192.168.1.10", LocalPort: 54321, RemoteIP: "8.8.8.8", RemotePort: 443, State: "ESTABLISHED", IsOutgoing: true},
	}
	s.Update(conns)
	snap := s.GetSnapshot(10)
	if snap.TotalConns != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 2 total, got %d", snap.TotalConns)
	}
	if snap.IncomingTotal != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 incoming, got %d", snap.IncomingTotal)
	}
	if snap.IncomingNew != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 1 incoming new, got %d", snap.IncomingNew)
	}
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/state/store.go
================================================================================
package state

import (
	"sort"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type Config struct {
	MaxConns       int
	MaxIPStats     int
	MaxHistory     int
	SmoothingAlpha float64
}

type Store struct {
	mu sync.RWMutex

	cfg Config

	connections map[string]*models.Connection
	ipStats     map[string]*models.IPStat
	stateStats  map[string]int

	history *History

	smoothedAvg   float64
	prevMovingAvg float64

	totalScans    int
	totalConns    int
	totalNew      int
	incomingTotal int
	incomingNew   int
}

type Snapshot struct {
	Time       time.Time
	TotalScans int
	TotalConns int
	TotalNew   int

	IncomingTotal int // сумма всех входящих соединений в newMap
	IncomingNew   int // новые входящие соединения за этот скан
	SmoothedAvg   float64
	MovingAvg     float64
	PrevMovingAvg float64

	StateStats map[string]int
	TopIPs     []models.TopIP
	IPStats    map[string]*models.IPStat
}

func NewStore(cfg Config) *Store {
	if cfg.MaxConns <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxConns = 10000
	}

	if cfg.MaxIPStats <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxIPStats = 10000
	}

	if cfg.MaxHistory <= 0 {
		cfg.MaxHistory = 720
	}

	if cfg.SmoothingAlpha <= 0 || cfg.SmoothingAlpha >= 1 {
		cfg.SmoothingAlpha = 0.3
	}

	return &Store{
		cfg:         cfg,
		connections: make(map[string]*models.Connection),
		ipStats:     make(map[string]*models.IPStat),
		stateStats:  make(map[string]int),
		history:     NewHistory(cfg.MaxHistory),
	}
}

func (s *Store) Update(conns []*models.Connection) {
	now := time.Now()

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	prevMoving := s.history.AverageLast(5)

	newMap := make(map[string]*models.Connection, len(conns))

	for _, c := range conns {
		if c == nil {
			continue
		}

		key := c.Key()

		old, exists := s.connections[key]
		if exists {
			c.FirstSeen = old.FirstSeen
			c.Count = old.Count + 1
		} else {
			c.FirstSeen = now
			c.Count = 1
		}

		c.LastSeen = now
		newMap[key] = c
	}

	if s.cfg.MaxConns > 0 && len(newMap) > s.cfg.MaxConns {
		pruneConnections(newMap, s.cfg.MaxConns)
	}

	newCount := 0
	incomingTotal := 0
	incomingNew := 0

	for key, c := range newMap {
		if c.IsIncoming {
			incomingTotal++
		}
		if _, ok := s.connections[key]; !ok {
			newCount++
			if c.IsIncoming {
				incomingNew++
			}
		}
	}

	stateStats := make(map[string]int)
	ipStats := make(map[string]*models.IPStat)

	for _, c := range newMap {
		stateStats[c.State]++

		st, ok := ipStats[c.RemoteIP]
		if !ok {
			st = &models.IPStat{
				IP:         c.RemoteIP,
				Ports:      make(map[int]struct{}),
				LocalPorts: make(map[int]struct{}),
				FirstSeen:  c.FirstSeen,
				LastSeen:   c.LastSeen,
			}
			ipStats[c.RemoteIP] = st
		}

		st.Count++

		// Направление трафика
		if c.IsIncoming {
			st.IncomingConns++
			st.LocalPorts[c.LocalPort] = struct{}{}
			if c.State == "SYN_RECV" {
				st.SynRecv++
				st.SynCount++
			}
		}
		if c.IsOutgoing {
			st.OutgoingConns++
			st.Ports[c.RemotePort] = struct{}{}
			if c.State == "SYN_SENT" {
				st.SynSent++
			}
		}

		// Fallback: если направление не определено (P2P), пишем в Ports
		if !c.IsIncoming && !c.IsOutgoing {
			st.Ports[c.RemotePort] = struct{}{}
		}

		if c.FirstSeen.Before(st.FirstSeen) {
			st.FirstSeen = c.FirstSeen
		}
		if c.LastSeen.After(st.LastSeen) {
			st.LastSeen = c.LastSeen
		}
	}

	if s.cfg.MaxIPStats > 0 && len(ipStats) > s.cfg.MaxIPStats {
		pruneIPStats(ipStats, s.cfg.MaxIPStats)
	}

	current := len(newMap)

	if s.totalScans == 0 {
		s.smoothedAvg = float64(current)
	} else {
		s.smoothedAvg = s.cfg.SmoothingAlpha*float64(current) +
			(1-s.cfg.SmoothingAlpha)*s.smoothedAvg
	}

	s.history.Add(current)

	s.connections = newMap
	s.ipStats = ipStats
	s.stateStats = stateStats
	s.totalConns = current
	s.totalNew = newCount
	s.incomingTotal = incomingTotal
	s.incomingNew = incomingNew
	s.prevMovingAvg = prevMoving
	s.totalScans++
}

func (s *Store) GetSnapshot(topLimit int) Snapshot {
	s.mu.RLock()
	defer s.mu.RUnlock()

	stateStats := make(map[string]int, len(s.stateStats))
	for k, v := range s.stateStats {
		stateStats[k] = v
	}

	// Глубокая копия: IPStat и вложенные мапы больше не шарятся со Store.
	ipStats := make(map[string]*models.IPStat, len(s.ipStats))
	for k, v := range s.ipStats {
		if v == nil {
			continue
		}
		cp := *v
		if v.Ports != nil {
			ports := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.Ports))
			for p := range v.Ports {
				ports[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.Ports = ports
		}
		if v.LocalPorts != nil {
			lp := make(map[int]struct{}, len(v.LocalPorts))
			for p := range v.LocalPorts {
				lp[p] = struct{}{}
			}
			cp.LocalPorts = lp
		}
		ipStats[k] = &cp
	}

	top := make([]models.TopIP, 0, len(ipStats))

	for _, st := range ipStats {
		top = append(top, models.TopIP{
			IP:        st.IP,
			Count:     st.Count,
			Ports:     len(st.Ports),
			FirstSeen: st.FirstSeen,
			LastSeen:  st.LastSeen,
		})
	}

	sort.Slice(top, func(i, j int) bool {
		if top[i].Count != top[j].Count {
			return top[i].Count > top[j].Count
		}

		if top[i].Ports != top[j].Ports {
			return top[i].Ports > top[j].Ports
		}

		return top[i].IP < top[j].IP
	})

	if topLimit > 0 && len(top) > topLimit {
		top = top[:topLimit]
	}

	return Snapshot{
		Time:          time.Now(),
		TotalScans:    s.totalScans,
		TotalConns:    s.totalConns,
		TotalNew:      s.totalNew,
		IncomingTotal: s.incomingTotal,
		IncomingNew:   s.incomingNew,
		SmoothedAvg:   s.smoothedAvg,
		MovingAvg:     s.history.AverageLast(5),
		PrevMovingAvg: s.prevMovingAvg,
		StateStats:    stateStats,
		TopIPs:        top,
		IPStats:       ipStats,
	}
}

func pruneConnections(m map[string]*models.Connection, max int) {
	if len(m) <= max {
		return
	}

	type item struct {
		key   string
		first time.Time
	}

	items := make([]item, 0, len(m))

	for k, v := range m {
		items = append(items, item{
			key:   k,
			first: v.FirstSeen,
		})
	}

	sort.Slice(items, func(i, j int) bool {
		if !items[i].first.Equal(items[j].first) {
			return items[i].first.After(items[j].first)
		}

		return items[i].key < items[j].key
	})

	for i := max; i < len(items); i++ {
		delete(m, items[i].key)
	}
}

func pruneIPStats(m map[string]*models.IPStat, max int) {
	if len(m) <= max {
		return
	}

	type item struct {
		ip    string
		count int
	}

	items := make([]item, 0, len(m))

	for ip, st := range m {
		items = append(items, item{
			ip:    ip,
			count: st.Count,
		})
	}

	sort.Slice(items, func(i, j int) bool {
		if items[i].count != items[j].count {
			return items[i].count > items[j].count
		}

		return items[i].ip < items[j].ip
	})

	for i := max; i < len(items); i++ {
		delete(m, items[i].ip)
	}
}

================================================================================
FILE: internal/storage/enrichment_jsonl.go
================================================================================
package storage

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/threatintel"
)

// EnrichmentJSONLStore сохраняет результаты Threat Intelligence в JSONL.
type EnrichmentJSONLStore struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	path string
}

// NewEnrichmentJSONLStore создает хранилище результатов обогащения.
func NewEnrichmentJSONLStore(dir string) (*EnrichmentJSONLStore, error) {
	if dir == "" {
		dir = "data"
	}

	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &EnrichmentJSONLStore{
		path: filepath.Join(dir, "threatintel.jsonl"),
	}, nil
}

type enrichmentAlertRecord struct {
	Time     string `json:"time"`
	IP       string `json:"ip"`
	Type     string `json:"type"`
	Severity string `json:"severity"`
	Details  string `json:"details"`
}

type enrichmentRecord struct {
	Time    string                `json:"time"`
	Alert   enrichmentAlertRecord `json:"alert"`
	Cached  bool                  `json:"cached"`
	Skipped bool                  `json:"skipped"`
	Reason  string                `json:"reason,omitempty"`
	Error   string                `json:"error,omitempty"`
	Report  *threatintel.IPReport `json:"report,omitempty"`
}

// StoreEnrichment сохраняет один результат обогащения.
func (s *EnrichmentJSONLStore) StoreEnrichment(
	ctx context.Context,
	res threatintel.EnrichmentResult,
) error {
	if s == nil {
		return nil
	}

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.OpenFile(s.path, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()

	record := enrichmentRecord{
		Time: time.Now().Format(time.RFC3339),
		Alert: enrichmentAlertRecord{
			Time:     res.OriginalAlert.Time.Format(time.RFC3339),
			IP:       res.OriginalAlert.IP,
			Type:     string(res.OriginalAlert.Type),
			Severity: res.OriginalAlert.Severity.String(),
			Details:  res.OriginalAlert.Details,
		},
		Cached:  res.Cached,
		Skipped: res.Skipped,
		Reason:  res.Reason,
		Report:  res.Report,
	}

	if res.Err != nil {
		record.Error = res.Err.Error()
	}

	return json.NewEncoder(file).Encode(record)
}


================================================================================
FILE: internal/storage/jsonl.go
================================================================================
package storage

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alt-dosguard/internal/models"
)

type AlertJSONLStore struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	path string
}

func NewAlertJSONLStore(dir string) (*AlertJSONLStore, error) {
	if dir == "" {
		dir = "data"
	}

	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &AlertJSONLStore{
		path: filepath.Join(dir, "alerts.jsonl"),
	}, nil
}

type alertRecord struct {
	Time     string `json:"time"`
	IP       string `json:"ip"`
	Type     string `json:"type"`
	Severity string `json:"severity"`
	Details  string `json:"details"`
}

func (s *AlertJSONLStore) AppendAlert(ctx context.Context, a models.Alert) (err error) {
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.OpenFile(s.path, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// Закрытие файла на записи: ошибка Close = возможная потеря данных,
	// поэтому не игнорируем, а возвращаем, если выше не было другой ошибки.
	defer func() {
		if cerr := file.Close(); cerr != nil && err == nil {
			err = fmt.Errorf("close jsonl file: %w", cerr)
		}
	}()

	rec := alertRecord{
		Time:     a.Time.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
		IP:       a.IP,
		Type:     string(a.Type),
		Severity: a.Severity.String(),
		Details:  a.Details,
	}

	return json.NewEncoder(file).Encode(rec)
}

func (s *AlertJSONLStore) QueryAlerts(ctx context.Context, limit int) ([]models.Alert, error) {
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.Open(s.path)
	if err != nil {
		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return nil, nil
		}
		return nil, err
	}

	// Чтение: ошибка Close не критична — закрываем и идём дальше.
	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()

	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 1024*1024), 1024*1024)

	var out []models.Alert

	for scanner.Scan() {
		var rec alertRecord

		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &rec); err != nil {
			continue
		}

		t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, rec.Time)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}

		out = append(out, models.Alert{
			Time:     t,
			IP:       rec.IP,
			Type:     models.AlertType(rec.Type),
			Severity: models.ParseSeverity(rec.Severity),
			Details:  rec.Details,
		})

		if limit > 0 && len(out) > limit {
			out = out[1:]
		}
	}

	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return out, nil
}

func (s *AlertJSONLStore) Close() error {
	return nil
}
